### [1] [Security and Human-Centered Assessment of BACnet-Controlled DALI Infrastructure in an Educational Building Automation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17089)
*Ariton Verush*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文以2026年瑞士图恩的楼宇自动化黑客马拉松为背景，对BACnet/IP与DALI照明基础设施组成的建筑自动化测试床进行了安全与人因案例研究。通过Yabe和BACteria等工具进行网络枚举、对象级检查、物理机架分析及反思性人机交互分析，重建了BACnet对象层次结构、识别了房间级照明控制路径，并建立了BACnet对象到DALI组级基础设施的映射。研究表明，BACS评估不仅是技术协议任务，还涉及可用工具界面、物理可观测性、可解释的命名约定及安全的心智模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化与控制系统(BACS)集成了暖通空调、照明、传感和管理功能，形成了复杂的网络物理环境，难以检查、安全防护和向新分析师解释。现有研究缺乏结合安全性、人因与教育视角的BACnet/DALI实测案例，特别是在可控教育测试床中的系统性探索。

Method: 在2026年4月瑞士图恩的楼宇自动化黑客马拉松中，对配备DALI照明基础设施的BACnet/IP测试床进行案例研究。综合运用Yabe和BACteria等工具，开展网络枚举、对象级检查、物理机架分析以及反思性人机交互(HCI)分析，记录可观测的BACnet服务、重建对象层次结构、识别照明控制路径，并将BACnet对象映射到DALI组级基础设施。

Result: 成功记录了可观测的BACnet服务，重建了结构化的对象层次结构，识别了房间级照明控制路径，并建立了BACnet对象到DALI组级基础设施的映射关系。研究发现BACS评估不仅依赖技术协议知识，还高度依赖工具界面的可用性、物理层的可观测性、命名约定的可解释性以及对命令优先级的正确心智模型。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个在可控教育测试床中进行BACnet/DALI探索的紧凑案例研究，为网络安全教育、以人为中心的安全工具设计以及在网络物理建筑环境中进行负责任的实验提供了实践启示。

Abstract: Building automation and control systems integrate heating, ventilation, air conditioning, lighting, sensing, and management functions through specialized communication protocols. While this integration enables flexible building operation, it also creates complex cyber-physical environments that are difficult to inspect, secure, and explain to new analysts. This paper presents a practical security and human-centered case study of a BACnet/IP building automation testbed with DALI lighting infrastructure, investigated during a domotics-oriented cybersecurity hackathon in Thun, Switzerland in April 2026. The study combines network-oriented enumeration, object-level inspection, physical rack analysis, and reflective HCI analysis of tool-supported learning. Using Yabe and BACteria, the work documents observable BACnet services, reconstructs structured object hierarchies, identifies room-level lighting-control paths, and maps BACnet objects to DALI group-level infrastructure. The analysis emphasizes that BACS assessment is not only a technical protocol task: it also requires usable tool interfaces, physical observability, interpretable naming conventions, and safe mental models for command priorities. The paper contributes a compact case study of BACnet/DALI exploration in an educational testbed and discusses implications for cybersecurity education, human-centered security tooling, and responsible experimentation in cyber-physical building environments.

</details>


### [2] [An AI Security Agent for Banking: Multi-Vector Fraud and AML Detection Across Retail and Corporate Accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17555)
*Joseph Walusimbi,Joshua Benjamin Ssentongo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向零售与公司银行的三组件融合AI安全代理，并行处理交易流与会话流，结合LSTM序列模型、统计速度/阈值监控和图网络模块，在13类威胁数据上比规则基线(F1提升22.5%/13.4%)和纯LSTM基线表现更优，并附带验证聊天机器人(96.6%身份验证准确率)和分析师辅助工具(99.3%行动推荐F1)，关键威胁自动响应延迟低于0.43ms(95分位)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行同时面临基于签名的欺诈(如卡不存在攻击、账户接管、ATM克隆)和基于行为金融犯罪(如结构化交易、洗钱分层、资金骡网络、商业电子邮件欺诈)，这两类威胁的检测需求根本不同。静态规则引擎能可靠捕获暴力攻击和高频事件，但对BEC支付重定向、会话劫持和洗钱分层等精心伪装成正常活动的威胁在结构上无能为力，亟需更智能的检测方案。

Method: 提出三组件融合架构：(1) 双并行事件流——交易流(卡欺诈、ACH/电汇欺诈、AML类别)与会话流(账户接管、会话劫持、SIM卡交换、内部滥用)；(2) 每个流包含三个并行组件：LSTM序列模型(捕捉账户行为历史)、统计速度/阈值监控器、图/网络模块(捕捉账户-对手方关系模式如扇入、扇出、传递比用于洗钱检测)；(3) 附带客户交易验证聊天机器人和分析师案例摘要助手。

Result: 在包含237,669笔交易和113,508个会话(13个威胁类别、3,470个模拟账户)的合成事件日志上，提出模型在交易流F1=0.787、会话流F1=0.867，优于规则基线(0.562/0.733)和LSTM-only基线(0.655/0.713)。聊天机器人身份验证准确率96.6%，大规模重置攻击检测率86.8%；分析师助手行动推荐F1=99.3%；关键级别自动响应延迟95分位小于0.43ms。

Conclusion: 提出的融合架构有效弥补了规则引擎和纯序列模型在复杂金融犯罪检测上的不足，通过多模态信息融合(序列行为+统计异常+关系图)实现了对签名欺诈和行为金融犯罪的同时检测，且具备实时代理响应能力，验证了AI安全代理在银行场景中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Banks simultaneously face signature-based fraud (card-not-present attacks, account takeover, ATM cloning) and behavioural financial crime (structuring, layering, mule networks, business email compromise) -- two threat families with fundamentally different detection requirements. Static rule engines that reliably catch brute-force and high-velocity events are structurally blind to business-email-compromise (BEC) payment redirection, session hijacking, and money-laundering layering, which are engineered to appear indistinguishable from legitimate activity at the individual transaction or session level. This paper presents an AI security agent for retail and corporate banking that addresses this gap through a three-component fusion architecture operating on two parallel event streams: a transaction stream (card fraud, ACH/wire fraud, AML categories) and a session stream (account takeover, session hijacking, SIM-swap, insider abuse). Each stream combines an LSTM sequence model capturing per-account behavioural history, a statistical velocity/threshold monitor, and a graph/network module capturing account-counterparty relationship patterns (fan-in, fan-out, pass-through ratio) for money-laundering detection. Experiments on a synthetic event log of 237,669 transactions and 113,508 sessions across 13 threat categories and 3,470 simulated accounts demonstrate overall F1 of 0.787 (transaction stream) and 0.867 (session stream) for the proposed model, versus 0.562/0.733 for a rule-based baseline and 0.655/0.713 for an LSTM-only baseline. The agent includes a customer-facing transaction-verification chatbot (96.6% identity verification accuracy, 86.8% mass-reset attack detection) and an analyst case-summary assistant (99.3% action-recommendation F1), with Critical-tier automated response latency under 0.43 ms at the 95th percentile.

</details>


### [3] [Securing Multi-Agent GIS Systems: Risk Evaluation and Prompt Hardening Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17092)
*Kyle Gao,Pranavi Kotta,Linlin Xu,Jonathan Li,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向多智能体GIS系统的安全框架，通过模块化状态机编排、红队攻击测试和提示优化，在保持任务性能的同时提升系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统与GIS的集成带来了复杂的安全风险，现有方法缺乏系统性的风险识别、评估与缓解框架。

Method: (1) 模块化状态机编排框架，将智能体行为抽象为可复用组件；(2) 红队攻击框架，使用自适应攻击LLM和确定性裁判进行多轮攻击评估；(3) 提示优化框架，将提示视为结构化签名并注入对抗性示例。

Result: 在商业地理空间合作伙伴的智能体系统上进行了测试，提示优化在提升安全性的同时未降低任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够系统地提升多智能体GIS系统的安全性，且具有良好的可迁移性，可适应更广泛的智能体架构。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly integrated with geographic information systems (GIS), where multi-agent coordination enables complex conversational and spatial analysis but introduces security risks. This work presents a security-oriented framework for risk identification, evaluation, and mitigation in a multi-agent GIS system while maintaining adaptability to broader agentic architectures. We test the agentic system of a commercial geospatial partner while developing a modular state-machine-based orchestration framework that abstracts agent behavior into reusable components. We evaluate robustness using a red-teaming framework with an adaptive attacker LLM and a deterministic judge that produces binary outcomes with supporting rationales across multi-turn attacks. We further improve resilience with a prompt optimization framework that treats prompts as structured signatures and injects adversarial demonstrations, enabling systematic security improvements without degrading task performance.

</details>


### [4] [Anywhere, Any-Stymie: Remote Activation of Trojan Malware on LiDAR with Modulated Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17562)
*R. Spencer Hallyburton,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文首次揭示了LiDAR传感器中可被远程光信号触发的潜伏性固件恶意软件攻击面，该恶意软件能在部署后实时操纵点云数据，实现虚假目标注入和真实目标抑制，影响自动驾驶系统的安全决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现LiDAR感知流水线中一个此前未被探索的攻击面：嵌入固件的恶意软件在正常运行时保持休眠状态，可在部署后通过外部触发激活，无需在攻击时访问硬件或网络。

Method: 设计了能够进行底层点云操纵的恶意软件并嵌入LiDAR固件；开发了光学触发机制，通过向感知环境发送调制信号远程激活恶意软件；激活后实时执行点云操纵，实现虚假目标注入和真实目标抑制。

Result: 静态攻击有效距离达300英尺，记录的攻击场景车速达35英里/小时；注入的人形伪影可被SOTA 3D目标检测器语义检测到；在部署的战术自主车辆上展示了多种安全关键影响模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了在LiDAR传感器全生命周期（开发与部署）中需要更强的完整性保障机制。

Abstract: LiDAR sensors are widely deployed in autonomous systems for 3D perception and safety-critical decision-making. We identify a previously unexplored attack surface in which dormant malware embedded in the LiDAR sensing pipeline remains inactive during normal operation and can be externally triggered after deployment, without requiring access to sensor hardware or networking at attack time. To operationalize this threat, we design malware capable of low-level point-cloud manipulation and embed it into LiDAR firmware. This malware was developed in a closed research test environment with vendor technical support, rather than by exploiting an inherent production supply-chain vulnerability. To selectively trigger attack activation, we design and implement an optical trigger that remotely activates the malware by delivering a modulated signal into the sensing environment. Once triggered, the malware performs real-time point cloud manipulation, and we demonstrate false object injection and real object suppression on static and mobile victim platforms. Our evaluation first establishes attack feasibility, including static operation at 300~ft and recorded drive-by runs reaching 35~mph. We then illustrate quantitatively that injected person-like artifacts can remain semantically detectable by a state-of-the-art 3D object detector. Finally, we demonstrate multiple modes of safety-critical impact on a deployed tactical autonomous vehicle. Together, these results highlight the need for stronger integrity guarantees throughout the LiDAR sensor development and deployment pipeline.

</details>


### [5] [Timestamp-Aware Spatio-Temporal Graph Contrastive Learning for Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17109)
*Jianli Dai,Guangwei Wu,Jiacheng Li,Weiping Wang,An He,Xinjun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自监督图神经网络的网络入侵检测框架，利用真实时间戳构建时序图，结合E-GraphSAGE和LSTM编码器提取时空依赖，并通过多视图图对比学习（时空特征对比）以及梯度范数自适应加权策略，在四个NIDS数据集上达到与有监督SOTA相当的检测性能，同时保持高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-based NIDS方法大多将流量视为时间独立，忽略了攻击行为的演化特性；且依赖有监督或半监督学习，难以泛化到未见过的攻击。

Method: 1) 根据真实时间戳构建时序图序列；2) 采用E-GraphSAGE+LSTM编码器提取时空依赖（避免注意力机制的高开销）；3) 多视图图对比学习（时空特征三重对比）；4) 梯度范数自适应加权策略优化对比损失权重。

Result: 在四个带真实时间戳的NIDS数据集上，显著优于现有自监督方法，性能与有监督SOTA GNN方法相当，且计算效率高。

Conclusion: 自监督GNN框架结合真实时间戳信息能有效捕捉网络流量的时空演化模式，在不依赖标签的情况下实现接近有监督方法的检测性能，验证了自监督学习在NIDS领域的潜力。

Abstract: Given their effectiveness in modeling the relational structure among network traffic flows, graph neural networks (GNNs) have been widely adopted in network intrusion detection systems (NIDSs). However, most existing GNN-based NIDS approaches focus on the relational structure of traffic flows, and treat them as temporally independent, which limits their ability to cope with evolving attack behaviors. Moreover, their reliance on supervised or semi-supervised learning often restricts generalization to unseen attacks. To address these limitations, we propose a novel self-supervised GNN-based framework. To the best of our knowledge, the proposed model is among the first self-supervised GNN-based NIDS models to explicitly leverage real timestamps, which provides faithful temporal dependencies for representation learning. We first construct a series of temporal graphs from network traffic flows according to their timestamps, and then employ an E-GraphSAGE and LSTM based encoder to fully extract temporal information and spatial dependencies of network traffic, without introducing time-costly attention mechanisms. A multi-view graph contrastive learning (GCL) scheme is introduced, where temporal, spatial, and feature contrasts are jointly performed to capture temporal continuity, preserve structural consistency, and improve the generalization and robustness of the learned representations, respectively. In addition, a gradient-norm-based adaptive weighting strategy is designed to optimize the contrastive loss weights. Experimental results on four representative NIDS datasets with real timestamps demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing self-supervised approaches and achieves performance comparable to the supervised state-of-the-art GNN method, while maintaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [6] [Loss Landscape Poisoning: Targeted Extraction of Unseen Training Data from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17110)
*Md Abdullah Al Mamun,Ngoc Phu Doan,Pedram Zaree,Ihsen Alouani,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据投毒攻击方法，通过重塑模型局部损失景观，使模型强制记忆目标记录，从而在集中式和联邦学习场景中实现高达100%的隐私泄露提取，并展示了一种能够绕过差分隐私防护的新攻击手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在私有或敏感数据上训练时面临被提取攻击的风险，现有研究尚未充分探讨攻击者通过投毒部分训练数据来泄露其无法访问的独立目标记录的可能性。

Method: 攻击者通过投毒数据在目标完成处创建一个尖锐的损失最小值，同时抬升附近替代序列的损失值，迫使模型将目标序列视为邻域内唯一的低损失解，从而强制模型记忆该目标。该方法无需修改模型架构，适用于集中式和联邦学习设置。

Result: 在语言模型上实现高达100%的成功提取率，在视觉-语言模型上实现高达90%的成功提取率。差分隐私训练能够抵御该攻击，但作者进一步提出一种直接探测损失景观的新攻击，能够绕过差分隐私防御。

Conclusion: 数据投毒可以显著放大隐私泄露风险，差分隐私虽能提供一定防护但并非绝对安全，需警惕更高级的基于损失景观探测的绕过攻击。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly trained on proprietary or sensitive data, from private healthcare and financial records to user conversations containing secrets. Ensuring the privacy of such data against extraction attacks has become a central concern. In this paper, we ask whether an attacker who can poison a portion of the training data can facilitate the leakage of a separate target record they have no access to. We answer in the affirmative and show that such leakage can be induced by a poisoning mechanism that reshapes the model's local loss landscape around the target completion. Our key insight is that poisoning to create a sharp loss minimum at the target, surrounded by elevated loss on nearby alternatives, forces the model to memorize the target as the unique low-loss solution in its neighborhood. The attack requires no architectural changes, and generalizes across centralized and federated learning settings. We demonstrate that the attack amplifies privacy leakage across language (up to 100% successful extraction), and vision-language models (up 90% successful extraction). We show that the attack is thwarted when the model is trained to be differentially private. However, we introduce a new attack that directly probes the loss landscape bypassing even differential privacy defenses.

</details>


### [7] [Learning Red Agent Policy from Observations for Neurosymbolic Autonomous Cyber Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18223)
*Ankita Samaddar,Sandeep Neema,Daniel Balasubramanian,Xenofon Koutsoukos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模仿学习的策略学习技术，用于预测部分可观测自主网络环境中红方（攻击者）的动作，从而辅助神经符号蓝方（防御者）智能体提升防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络面临复杂网络攻击，需要基于强化学习的自主防御智能体。然而，网络环境是部分可观测系统，防御者无法观测攻击者的动作，难以预测攻击者策略、评估入侵程度，因此需要一种能够从观测中学习攻击者策略的方法。

Method: 采用模仿学习技术，针对具有离散状态和离散动作的部分可观测强化学习智能体进行策略学习。该方法从网络观测和防御者动作中预测红方动作，并集成到神经符号网络防御智能体中。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理不同的红方策略，在多种模拟场景下实现了高预测准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的模仿学习策略学习技术能有效预测部分可观测网络环境中攻击者的动作，提升神经符号防御智能体的性能，为自主网络防御提供支持。

Abstract: With sophisticated cyber-attacks becoming increasingly prevalent, modern networks require intelligent autonomous cyber-defense agents trained via Reinforcement Learning (RL). These agents employ neurosymbolic approaches such as behavior trees with learning-enabled components (LECs) to learn, reason, adapt, and implement security rules while maintaining critical operations. However, these autonomous networks are partially observable systems, i.e., the cyber-attacker's (red agent's) actions are not observable, making it difficult for the defender to predict red actions, learn red policies, or assess the attacker's intrusion levels. To address this, we propose a Policy Learning Technique using imitation learning to learn policies for partially observable RL agents with discrete states and discrete actions. We apply this technique in an autonomous cyber environment to predict red agent's actions from network observations and defender actions. Integrated with a neurosymbolic cyber-defense agent, our method effectively handles different red policies and achieves high prediction accuracy across diverse simulated scenarios.

</details>


### [8] [Fractional Verkle Trees: A Hypertree Decomposition and Verified Proof Serialization Architecture for High-Performance Blockchain State Accumulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17111)
*Ekleen Kaur,Everton Fraga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对以太坊Verkle树实现中的四种低效问题，提出分数Verkle树（FVT）——一种将全局状态分解为N个独立子累加器的超树分解方案，通过存在性检查、32字节SHA256节点引用、零拷贝引用计数缓冲区和基于哈希表的字典序去重等优化，实现57%堆分配减少、并行插入2,433 ns/op，并在全网6000个全节点上每年消除4.85 PB数据，推动以太坊无状态化路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链状态管理面临关键可扩展性瓶颈：维护数亿条目的密码学承诺在计算上变得不可承受。以太坊转向Verkle树（多项式承诺累加器，通过常数大小的IPA向量承诺将证明大小从O(width*depth)降至O(depth)），但当前实现在运行中表现出加重家庭验证者负担的病态特征。

Method: 提出分数Verkle树（FVT），采用超树分解将全局状态划分为N个独立子累加器，由Merkle承诺树协调。针对四种低效问题分别提出：存在性检查避免幻影节点创建、32字节SHA256节点引用替代64字节数据库键以减少LSM树压缩、零拷贝引用计数缓冲区消除冗余内存复制、基于哈希表的字典序去重解决证明序列化非确定性。

Result: 在Apple M1 Pro上，每10K证明堆分配从566,760字节降至242,004字节（减少57%）；并行插入达2,433 ns/op；根重计算仅需91 μs（对比约500 ms）；全网6000个全节点每年消除4.85 PB数据。

Conclusion: FVT通过超树分解和针对性优化显著提升了Verkle树实现的效率，推动以太坊无状态化路线图的实现。

Abstract: Modern blockchain state management faces a critical scalability bottleneck: maintaining cryptographic commitments over hundreds of millions of entries becomes computationally prohibitive. Ethereum's transition to Verkle Trees: polynomial commitment accumulators reducing proof sizes from O(width * depth) to O(depth) via constant-size IPA vector commitments, is a critical step toward stateless operation. Yet, current implementations exhibit pathological characteristics that burden home validators.
  We identify four inefficiencies in the reference go-verkle implementation \cite{kaur2025goverkle, kaur2025goethereum}: (1) phantom node creation during non-existent account deletion; (2) 64-byte database keys triggering excessive LSM-tree compaction; (3) redundant memory copying in proof deserialization; (4) a Proof of Absence wire format incompatibility causing non-deterministic serialization.
  We present Fractional Verkle Trees (FVT), a hypertree decomposition partitioning global state into N independent sub-accumulators coordinated by a Merkle commitment tree, achieving improved cache locality, zero-lock-contention goroutine-parallel commitment computation, and faster root recomputation (91 $μ$s vs $\sim$500 ms). We address each inefficiency via existence checks, 32-byte SHA256 node references, zero-copy reference-counted buffers, and HashMap-based lexicographic deduplication.
  Benchmarks on Apple M1 Pro show 57\% heap allocation reduction (566,760 to 242,004 bytes per 10K proofs), parallel insertion at 2,433 ns/op, and network-wide elimination of 4.85 PB/year across 6,000 full nodes, advancing the Ethereum stateless roadmap.

</details>


### [9] [An Evaluation of Data Leakage Risks in Tool-Using LLM Agents in Realistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17114)
*Hankyul Baek,Jaewon Noh,Sang Seo,Yongsu Kim,Gabriel Waikin Loh Matienzo,Young Il Kim,Ee Wei Seah,Akriti Vij*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文由新加坡AI安全研究所与韩国AI安全研究所联合开展，评估了AI Agent在12个非对抗性真实任务中的数据泄露风险，涵盖客户支持、DevOps、Web自动化及企业/个人生产力场景。研究发现，所有测试的Agent均未能在完全正确执行任务的同时保证数据安全，成功完成任务常伴随数据泄露（如访问不必要信息或向不恰当接收者披露信息），表明能力与数据安全应分开评估。研究识别出五种风险类型，并揭示了声明-行动不匹配、模拟感知行为等质量问题。结论认为操作型数据泄露是区别于对抗性数据窃取的一阶Agent安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注通过提示注入和越狱等对抗性方式导致的数据泄露，但非对抗性使用场景下敏感信息也可能意外暴露。因此，作者希望通过系统化评估揭示非对抗性任务中Agent数据泄露的风险程度和特征。

Method: 联合设计了12个真实、非对抗性任务场景，涵盖客户支持、DevOps、Web自动化、企业及个人生产力五大领域。评估覆盖五种风险类型：缺乏数据意识、受众意识、政策合规性、数据最小化及访问边界意识。两家机构使用独立测试环境和任务特定LLM评判标准对同一组场景进行测试。

Result: 所有被测试的Agent均未能在所有场景中实现完全正确且安全的执行。成功完成任务常伴随数据处理失败（如访问不必要信息或向不恰当接收者透露信息）。定性审查还揭示了声明-行动不匹配、模拟感知行为、用户模拟器角色反转以及自动评判中的解释差距等问题。

Conclusion: 操作型数据泄露是与对抗性数据外泄不同的一阶Agent安全问题，能力和数据处理安全应分开评估。该研究为未来评估Agent数据处理安全性提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being adopted in enterprise and personal settings with access to emails, databases, documents, and other tools where they can read, update, and disseminate sensitive information. Much of prior research on data leakage risks in agents has focused on adversarial data exfiltration through prompt injections and jailbreaks. However, sensitive information may also be exposed during non-adversarial use, creating leakage risks even when users issue benign requests.
  We report a joint evaluation by the Singapore AI Safety Institute and the Korea AI Safety Institute examining agent data leakage in 12 realistic, non-adversarial tasks spanning customer support, DevOps, web automation, and enterprise and personal productivity. The evaluation covers five risk types: lack of data awareness, audience awareness, policy compliance, data minimization, and access-boundary awareness. Both institutes tested a common set of scenarios mirroring real-world deployments using independent testing environments and task-specific LLM-judge rubrics.
  Across the three tested agents, none achieved fully correct and fully safe execution across all scenarios. Successful task completion often coincided with data-handling failures such as accessing unnecessary information or disclosing information to inappropriate recipients, indicating that capability and data-handling safety should be evaluated separately. Qualitative review also revealed claim-action mismatches, simulation-aware behavior, user-simulator role reversal, and interpretation gaps in automated judging. Overall, the results indicate that operational data leakage is a first-order agent-safety concern distinct from adversarial exfiltration and provide a methodology for future evaluations of agent data-handling safety.

</details>


### [10] [Quantifying quantum risk: a measure of crypto agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17116)
*Coryan Wilson-Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"轮转时间"（rotation time）作为衡量密码敏捷性（crypto agility）的指标，并推导出轮转时间容差与安全风险容差之间的近似关系。利用历史CVE数据计算出轮转时间容差的示例值在数小时到数天之间，表明结合密码敏捷性与混合加密是设计量子弹性系统的有效方法，但满足组织风险容差可能需要具有挑战性的技术和操作容差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机对当前广泛使用的密码算法构成威胁，尽管实用化的量子计算机可能在十年内出现，但"先收割，后解密"的攻击者行为模式要求现在就必须采取缓解措施。现有文献在推导此类系统的容差方面存在关键空白。

Method: 提出"轮转时间"概念作为密码敏捷性的度量，推导轮转时间容差与安全风险容差之间的近似关系，并使用历史CVE数据计算轮转时间容差的示例值。

Result: 轮转时间容差的示例值在数小时到数天之间。

Conclusion: 使用密码敏捷性与混合加密相结合是设计量子弹性系统的有效方法，但可能需要挑战性的技术和操作容差以满足组织风险容差。

Abstract: Because of their ability to enable new forms of cryptanalysis, quantum computers pose a threat to the cryptographic algorithms that are widely used to secure contemporary computer systems. A practical quantum computer may emerge within the next ten years or so, but due to theorised "harvest now, decrypt later" style attacker behaviour, mitigations are necessary today. Recent advances in cryptography and security architecture show promise in supporting the design of systems that exhibit resilience against quantum-enabled cryptanalysis, however there is a key gap in the literature around the subject of deriving tolerances for such systems. In this paper, we introduce the concept of rotation time as a measure of crypto agility, and derive an approximation that links rotation time tolerance to security risk tolerance. Historical CVE data is used to calculate illustrative values for rotation time tolerance, which is found to be of the order of hours to days. This demonstrates that using crypto agility in conjunction with hybrid encryption is an effective approach for designing quantum-resilient systems, but may necessitate challenging technical and operational tolerances in order to meet organisational risk tolerances.

</details>


### [11] [Graph neural networks at war: integrating cybersecurity and drone intelligence in the Israeli-Iranian conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17119)
*Sozan Sulaiman Maghdid,Tarik Ahmed Rashid,Shavan Askar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用图神经网络（GNNs）增强包含网络入侵和无人机的信息物理系统的网络安全与无人机管理，提出集成图学习方法用于入侵检测和无人机响应，基于仿真案例验证了该方法在态势感知、集群协调和自适应机动方面的有效性，检测率达94.2%，AUC为0.955，平均响应时间1.4秒，且GraphSAGE网络优于GCN和GAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统面临检测与即时响应的新威胁和挑战，需要更有效的方法来应对网络入侵并协调无人机等物理设备的响应。

Method: 提出集成图神经网络（GNN）的流程，使入侵检测系统学习底层网络结构、识别恶意活动，并驱动无人机响应措施；通过仿真案例构建网络攻击模型以激发无人机响应，采用GraphSAGE网络进行图学习。

Result: 检测率94.2%，平均AUC为0.955，平均响应时间1.4秒；GraphSAGE网络在相同情境下优于图卷积网络（GCN）和图注意力网络（GAT）。

Conclusion: 图神经网络可有效用于动态信息物理系统的入侵防范与响应。

Abstract: Physical cyber systems have brought about new threats and challenges in detection and immediate response. This study examines how Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) can be used to aid cybersecurity and drone management in a physical cyber system comprising of cyber intrusions and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). By providing a bridge between structural understanding of graphical neural networks, this work has provided an integrated procedure that allows intrusion detection systems to educate on underlying network structures, identify malicious activity, and facilitates drone response measures. Based on an emulation-based case study, cyberattacks models were created to provoke the responses of the drones, which proved that graph-based learning can assist with the situational awareness, swarm coordination, and adaptive maneuver. According to the performance valuation, this method has a detection rate of 94.2, average area under the receiver operating characteristic (ROC) of 0.955 and an average response time of 1.4 seconds. Comparative experiments reveal that proposed GraphSAGE network is more effective than the Graphical Convolutional Networks (GCNs) and Graphical Attention Networks (GATs) in the identical situation. Such findings prove that graphical neural networks can be used to avert intrusion and response of dynamic cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [12] [TrustErase: Auditable Instant Machine Unlearning with Passport-Embedded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17122)
*Rutger Hendrix,Leonardo G. Russo,Concetto Spampinato,Matteo Pennisi,Giovanni Bellitto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustErase 是一种基于护照嵌入的可验证、无数据遗忘框架，通过参数高效适配层中的护照密钥实现即时、模块化、可审计的遗忘，无需重训练或原始数据访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私合规AI需求日益增长，但现有基于重训练或蒸馏的机器遗忘方法存在不可验证且计算成本高的问题。

Method: 将护照（passport）视为参数高效适配层中的加密密钥，通过奇异值分解将护照嵌入模型权重；遗忘时仅需停用对应护照，无需重训练、微调或原始数据。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR10和CIFAR100上，TrustErase匹配或超越DELETE、L2UL、Boundary Shrink等基准方法，且完全在无数据条件下运行。

Conclusion: TrustErase为可信、可问责、可即时遗忘的AI系统建立了新范式。

Abstract: The demand for privacy-compliant AI has amplified the need for machine unlearning; yet, existing retraining or distillation-based methods remain unverifiable and computationally costly. We introduce TrustErase, a verifiable, data-free unlearning framework leveraging passport-embedded representations for instant, modular, and auditable forgetting. By treating passports as cryptographic keys within parameter-efficient adaptation layers, TrustErase enables the removal of specific classes or datasets through simple deactivation, without retraining, fine-tuning, or access to the original data. A singular value based decomposition conceals passports within model weights, ensuring that unlearning actions remain transparent and provably compliant. Evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR10 and CIFAR100 show that TrustErase matches or exceeds state-of-the-art benchmarks such as DELETE, L2UL, and Boundary Shrink, while operating in a strictly data-free regime. Ultimately, TrustErase establishes a new paradigm for trustworthy, accountable, and instantly forgettable AI systems.

</details>


### [13] [LineageMark: Multi-user White-box Watermarking for Contribution Tracing in Model Derivation Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17123)
*Bingxue Zhang,Xiaofeng Xu,Feida Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LineageMark，一种面向大语言模型多阶段推导链的多用户白盒水印框架，通过基于投影的方法在模型参数中编码水印，能够跨阶段保留贡献者水印并支持增量式多用户插入，对重水印、微调、量化和剪枝等扰动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印方法主要针对单用户一次性嵌入设计，在多阶段模型推导和增量更新场景下失效，无法满足开源大语言模型生态中对模型溯源和知识产权保护的追踪验证需求。

Method: 采用基于投影的水印编码方法：首先选择稳定载体以降低对模型变化的敏感性，每个水印位表示为这些载体上的投影统计量；额外水印插入仅在投影空间引入有界扰动，并利用边际约束保持信号完整性。

Result: 实验表明LineageMark能够在多阶段模型推导链中保留贡献者水印，支持增量式多用户水印插入，并对重水印、微调、量化、剪枝等扰动表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LineageMark有效解决了多用户、多阶段推导链场景下的水印问题，为开源LLM生态中的模型溯源和知识产权保护提供了可行的框架方案。

Abstract: In open large language model (LLM) ecosystems, models are frequently adapted across multiple domains and applications, forming multi-stage derivation chains. Consequently, tracking and verifying historical contributions is essential for model provenance and intellectual property protection. However, existing watermarking methods are mainly designed for single-user, one-time embeddings, often fail under repeated model derivation and incremental updates. To address this problem, we propose LineageMark, a multi-user white-box watermarking framework for model derivation chains. The framework encodes watermarks in model parameters using a projection-based approach. Stable carriers are first selected to reduce sensitivity to model changes, each watermark bit is then represented as a projection statistic over these carriers. Additional watermark insertions introduce only bounded perturbations in the projection space, and margin constraints are used to maintain signal integrity. We evaluate the effectiveness of LineageMark in multi-stage model derivation chains. Experimental results show that LineageMark preserves contributor watermarks across multi-stage derivation and supports incremental multi-user watermark insertion. Furthermore, it exhibits robustness against perturbations such as re-watermarking, fine-tuning, quantization, and pruning.

</details>


### [14] [Safety, Security, and Cognitive Risks in Neuro-Symbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17223)
*Manoj Parmar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地定义了神经符号AI（NeSy）的攻击面，提出跨层放大比等量化指标，并通过三个实证基准验证了符号层攻击（知识图谱投毒、推理引擎颠覆）的有效性与隐蔽性，揭示了现有检测方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI结合了神经感知与符号推理，在可解释性和结构化推理方面优势显著，但其混合架构引入了远超纯神经系统的攻击面（涵盖五个层次），现有安全研究缺乏对此类系统的系统性威胁建模与量化分析。

Method: (1) 形式化定义了NeSy攻击面、符号完整性违反（SIV）和跨层放大比；(2) 扩展MITRE ATLAS威胁模型，新增11种NeSy特定战术和五类攻击者画像；(3) 构建符号层威胁目录；(4) 分析认知风险的结构性放大机制；(5) 提出符合NIST AI 600-1和EU AI Act的多学科缓解措施；(6) 设计三个实证基准实验。

Result: (E1) 在205实体的医学知识图谱中，靶向投毒在注入预算B=5时达到盈亏平衡的SIV；(E2) PGD-10攻击在DistilBERT+ProbLog流水线上实现跨层放大比X=5.884；(E3) 单公理OWL编辑达到93.3%的SIV成功率且100%保持Pellet一致性，但STIX检测仅50%（随机猜测水平）。

Conclusion: 神经符号AI面临独特且严峻的安全挑战，符号层攻击高效隐蔽且跨层放大效应显著，现有检测方法严重不足，亟需新的防御机制与标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic AI (NeSy) pairs neural perception with symbolic reasoning, making it attractive for high-stakes domains where explainability and structured inference are required. However, this hybrid architecture introduces an enlarged attack surface spanning five layers: neural perception, symbolic knowledge bases, reasoning engines, agentic orchestration, and data stores -- each exploitable in ways absent from purely neural systems.
  This paper makes six contributions: (1) formal definitions of NeSy Attack Surface, Symbolic Integrity Violation (SIV), and Cross-Layer Amplification Ratio $\mathcal{X}$, decomposed into neural-caused and autonomous symbolic sensitivity components; (2) a unified threat model extending MITRE ATLAS with 11 NeSy-specific tactic extensions and a five-profile attacker taxonomy; (3) a symbolic-layer threat catalogue covering knowledge graph (KG) poisoning, ontology-merging, and inference-engine subversion; (4) analysis of cognitive risks -- automation bias, authority bias, and sycophantic reinforcement -- structurally amplified by NeSy's explicit logical explanations relative to black-box neural outputs; (5) interdisciplinary mitigations with measurable acceptance criteria aligned to NIST AI 600-1 and the EU AI Act; (6) three empirical benchmarks: (E1) targeted KG poisoning achieves break-even SIV at injection budget $B=5$ on a 205-entity medical KG, with a KG-specific stealth/SIV trade-off; (E2) PGD-10 at $\varepsilon=0.01$ yields $\mathcal{X}=5.884$ (95% CI $[4.64,\, 8.00]$, $p<0.0001$), confirmed adversarially specific by a matched-random baseline ($E^{R}_{\mathrm{rand}}=0$), on a DistilBERT+ProbLog pipeline; (E3) single-axiom OWL edits achieve 93.3% SIV success with 100% Pellet-consistency stealth, but held-out STIX detection fails at 50% (random-guessing level), an open problem.

</details>


### [15] [Cache to the Future: A Distributed Webpage Archive for Internet Blackouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17245)
*Ross Evans,Diogo Barradas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cache to the Future (CttF)系统，通过分布式社区评分、数字签名和工作量证明等机制，在互联网断网期间利用移动网状网络缓存和传递静态网页内容，实现城市规模的断网内容访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有断网抵抗技术主要依赖移动网状网络提供有限的消息服务，但缺乏能够在断网期间持续访问网络知识源（如静态网页内容）的技术方案。

Method: CttF系统采用分布式社区评分机制实现大规模缓存众包，结合数字签名和工作量证明等密码学构造来抵御对抗性干扰。

Result: 通过真实场景模拟验证，CttF能够在城市规模下、在多种良性及对抗性场景中有效传递静态内容。

Conclusion: CttF填补了断网期间静态网页内容访问的技术空白，为互联网断网环境下的知识传播提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Internet blackouts, occurring due to technological mishaps or intentional governmental action, prevent citizens from accessing the internet. Citizens in regions where internet blackouts are common have utilized blackout-resistant technologies to maintain communication. Such technologies often rely on mobile mesh networks to provide limited messaging services. However, no technology currently exists which can provide continued access to knowledge sources on the web during a blackout.
  We present Cache to the Future (CttF): a system to cache and deliver static content hosted on the web during a blackout. CttF's distributed community ratings crowdsources caching at scale while cryptographic constructs (digital signatures, proofs-of-work) mitigate adversarial interference. Our realistic simulations demonstrate CttF delivering content at city-scale across a wide range of benign and adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [16] [ARVO: Atlas of Reproducible Vulnerabilities for Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17283)
*Xiang Mei,Jordi Del Castillo,Pulkit Singh Singaria,Haoran Xi,Abdelouahab Benchikh,Tiffany Bao,Ruoyu Wang,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Adam Doupé,Hammond Pearce,Brendan Dolan-Gavitt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，通过克服大规模漏洞复现的关键障碍，为最大的开源软件漏洞数据集OSS-Fuzz引入了完整的可复现性，构建了ARVO数据集（包含超过6,100个真实漏洞，覆盖311个项目），实现了81%的漏洞复现成功率和89.4%的补丁定位准确率，打破了可复现性、数量和多样性之间固有的三重权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，漏洞数据集在可复现性、数量和多样性之间存在固有的三重权衡，其中可复现性往往被忽视，这限制了从历史漏洞数据集中自动提取信息的能力，降低了其对下游安全研究的实用价值。

Method: 识别大规模漏洞复现的关键障碍并提出通用解决方案，将可复现性引入最大的开源软件漏洞数据集OSS-Fuzz，构建ARVO数据集。该数据集以可复现性为核心，每个漏洞均可跨版本一致地重建、触发和分析，同时支持自动识别对应补丁以及与代码变更后的漏洞直接交互。

Result: ARVO数据集包含超过6,100个真实漏洞，覆盖311个项目，成功复现81%的漏洞，补丁定位准确率达89.4%。

Conclusion: ARVO打破了可复现性、数量和多样性之间的三重权衡，为下游安全研究提供了大规模、可复现的漏洞数据集，并对上游实践和下游安全研究产生了积极影响。

Abstract: Achieving reproducibility, quantity, and diversity in vulnerability datasets has long been viewed as an inherent three-way trade-off, where improving one dimension often comes at the cost of the others. In practice, reproducibility has been the dimension most often neglected. This has limited what can be automatically extracted from historical bug datasets, and has reduced their utility for downstream security research.
  In this work, we propose a method to produce a new security dataset which ensures reproducibility for diverse vulnerabilities at scale by identifying the key obstacles to large-scale bug reproduction and addressing them with general solutions. Using this method, we introduce full reproducibility to the largest open source software vulnerability dataset (OSS-Fuzz) and construct the ARVO dataset (an Atlas of Reproducible Vulnerabilities in Open-source software). ARVO is a large-scale dataset consisting of over 6,100 real-world vulnerabilities across 311 projects. Focusing on reproducibility, ARVO differs from existing datasets by providing each vulnerability in a form that can be consistently rebuilt, triggered, and analyzed across versions. Reproducibility also enables automatic identification of the corresponding patch for each vulnerability and supports direct interaction with vulnerabilities after code changes, capabilities that existing large-scale datasets do not provide. In our evaluation, ARVO successfully reproduces 81% of vulnerabilities and achieves 89.4% accuracy on the located patches. We also discuss ARVO's influence on both upstream practices and downstream security research.

</details>


### [17] [OTRO: Oblivious Tokenization Path with Square-Root ORAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17358)
*Jonghyun Lee,Yongqin Wang,Rachit Rajat,Daniel Wong,Mengyuan Li,Murali Annavaram*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OTRO是一种面向机密计算环境中LLM服务的高效 oblivious tokenization 路径，通过复制平方根ORAM实例、基于epoch的旋转策略和分块KV缓存感知tokenization三项创新，将tokenizer侧信道泄漏防护的开销从13倍降至TTFT增加不超过4.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU端LLM tokenizer是机密计算栈中的关键安全漏洞。tokenizer通过表驱动查找转换prompt，产生的内存访问模式构成强大的侧信道泄漏源。现有工作已证明可在Intel TDX上从tokenizer访问模式端到端恢复用户prompt。使用PathORAM等 oblivious 方案虽可防护但引入约13倍tokenizer减速，导致TTFT增加10-58%，无法满足延迟敏感的LLM服务需求。

Method: 1) 利用tokenizer表的只读特性，提供复制的平方根ORAM实例池，避免O(N log²N)重建开销；2) 基于epoch的旋转策略将访问与重建解耦，每个epoch边界填充dummy访问以最小化可观测信息；3) 分块KV缓存感知tokenization，使重建与GPU prefill重叠，减少实例数量。

Result: 在TDX启用的CVM和NVIDIA H100 GPU上，OTRO将TTFT开销限制在4.5%以内，tokenizer引起的延迟控制在总TTFT的10%以下，额外内存开销低于0.5 GB，同时显著减少了tokenizer的可观测泄漏。

Conclusion: OTRO通过三项关键创新有效解决了机密计算环境下LLM tokenizer的侧信道泄漏防护效率问题，在保证安全性的同时实现了极低延迟开销，适用于延迟敏感的LLM服务场景。

Abstract: The CPU-side large language model (LLM) tokenizer is a critical security gap in LLM serving through a confidential computing stack with CPU and GPU trusted execution environments (TEEs). Tokenizers converts the prompts through table-driven lookups, and the resulting memory access patterns are a powerful source of side-channel leakage. Recent work demonstrates end-to-end recovery of user prompts from tokenizer access pattern on production Intel TDX. However, a drop-in use of the popular tree-based Oblivious RAMs (e.g., PathORAM) to prevent access-pattern leakage introduces $\sim$13$\times$ tokenizer slowdown, resulting in 10-58% higher time-to-first-token (TTFT).
  In this paper, we present OTRO, an efficient, oblivious tokenization path tailored to latency-critical LLM serving. OTRO relies on square-root ORAM for fast single-access lookups, but avoids its prohibitive $O(N\log^2N$) rebuild cost every $\sqrt{N}$ accesses through three key innovations. First, OTRO provides a pool of replicated square-root ORAM instances that utilize the read-only nature of tokenizer table. Second, an epoch-based rotation policy decouples accesses from rebuilds and pads each epoch with dummy accesses to its boundaries, minimizing observable information. Lastly, chunked KV-cache-aware tokenization further overlaps rebuilds with GPU prefill and minimizes the instance count. Implemented as modules in HuggingFace Tokenizers and nano-vLLM, running within a TDX-enabled CVM with an NVIDIA H100 GPU, OTRO limits TTFT overhead to at most 4.5%, keeps tokenizer-induced latency under 10\% of total TTFT, and adds less than 0.5 GB of memory overhead while reducing the tokenizer's observable leakage across various model families and sizes.

</details>


### [18] [SoK: AI-Augmented Binary Reversing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17398)
*Yujeong Kwon,Yiyue Zhang,Shakhzod Yuldoshkhujaev,Kexin Pei,Dokyung Song,Hyungjoon Koo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了2015年以来AI增强二进制逆向领域的144篇研究论文，提出了统一的分类体系，涵盖22个逆向领域，并明确了LLM和智能体AI系统的新兴角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制逆向因编译过程中语义信息的不可逆丢失而极具挑战性。尽管AI、LLM和智能体系统加速了该领域发展，但相关工作在逆向领域、表示方法、学习范式和评估实践上日益碎片化，缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 分析了2015年以来的144篇研究论文，按推理任务将其组织为22个二进制逆向领域；提出了统一的分类体系，连接传统分析技术、二进制衍生工件、表示策略、学习范式和下游推理任务。

Result: 建立了涵盖传统和AI增强逆向流程的统一分类体系，揭示了不同方法背后的共性结构，识别了持续存在的技术挑战和评估缺口，并指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 通过建立通用词汇表和结构化框架，为下一代可靠且可扩展的AI增强二进制逆向系统提供了基础，阐明了该领域过去十年的演变全貌。

Abstract: Binary reversing is fundamental to software understanding, vulnerability discovery, malware investigation, and firmware auditing. However, it remains inherently challenging due to the irreversible loss of semantic information during compilation. Recent advances in machine learning, large language models (LLMs), and agentic AI systems have accelerated the adoption of AI-augmented binary reversing. Yet, the resulting body of work has become increasingly fragmented across reversing domains, artifact representations, learning approaches, and evaluation practices. This paper presents the first comprehensive systematization of knowledge on AI-augmented binary reversing. We analyze 144 research papers published since 2015, and organize them into 22 binary reversing domains according to the inference tasks. We further introduce a unified taxonomy spanning conventional and AI-augmented reversing pipelines. Our taxonomy connects traditional analysis techniques, binary-derived artifacts, representation strategies, learning paradigms, and downstream inference tasks, while clarifying the emerging roles of LLMs and agentic AI systems. By establishing a common vocabulary and structured framework, we provide a holistic view of the field's evolution over the past decade. Our study reveals common structures underlying seemingly disparate approaches, highlights persistent technical challenges and evaluation gaps, and identifies promising opportunities for future research. Collectively, these insights clarify the current state of the field and provide a foundation for the next generation of reliable and scalable AI-augmented binary reversing systems.

</details>


### [19] [Bifrost: Hybrid TEE-FHE Inference for Privacy-Preserving Transformer and LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17421)
*Chenghao Chen,Kailun Qin,Xiaolin Zhang,Chi Zhang,Dawu Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Bifrost提出了一种混合TEE-FHE架构，将机密仅配置给经过认证的CPU TEE，利用FHE作为安全委托机制在加速器上执行线性层，而非线性操作、KV状态转换及刷新等则在CPU TEE内执行。Bifrost+进一步采用prefill/decode分离，将提示端KV状态构建留在CPU TEE中。实验显示相比Euston方法延迟降低9.25-9.91倍，相比直接CKKS/FHE部署TTFT降低14.6-53.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管的大语言模型推理存在机密性问题：用户提示可能包含敏感代码、商业数据、个人信息或受监管文档，而远程服务会将中间状态暴露给云软件栈和加速器运行时。现有方案中，全同态加密（FHE）成本高昂且线性层与非线性、缓存状态及刷新敏感算子交织；CPU TEE可原生执行这些算子，但单独的CPU TEE无法定义不可信加速器的参与方式。

Method: Bifrost采用混合TEE-FHE架构：机密仅配置给经过认证的CPU TEE，加速器、设备内存、驱动/运行时栈及主机软件均不在可信计算基内。FHE作为安全委托机制用于加速器上的CKKS投影和前馈线性层，而非线性算子、注意力侧控制逻辑、KV状态转换及解密再加密刷新在CPU TEE内执行。Bifrost+进一步采用prefill/decode分离，提示端KV状态在CPU TEE内构建，仅解码端状态进入混合密文路径。

Result: 在匹配Euston方法的估算器风格对比中，Bifrost在GPT-2（1.5B）上降低投影延迟9.25倍，在LLaMA 3（8B）上降低9.91倍。在直接CKKS/FHE部署中，Bifrost+在GPT-2（124M）上降低TTFT 14.6-45.8倍，在Qwen3（0.6B）上降低15.3-53.4倍。

Conclusion: 选择性加密执行的核心经验：仅在需要仅密文加速器委托时使用FHE，而非线性操作、刷新操作及提示端工作保留在CPU TEE内。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted transformer and large language model (LLM) inference creates a direct confidentiality problem: user prompts may contain sensitive code, business data, personal information, or regulated documents, yet remote serving exposes intermediate state to the cloud software stack and accelerator runtime. Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) keeps accelerator-side execution ciphertext-only, but end-to-end LLM inference remains expensive because linear layers are interleaved with non-linear, cache-state, and refresh-sensitive operators. CPU trusted execution environments (TEEs) can execute those operators natively, but a CPU TEE alone does not define how an untrusted accelerator should participate.
  We present Bifrost, a hybrid TEE-FHE serving architecture in which secrets are provisioned only to an attested CPU TEE, while the accelerator, device memory, driver/runtime stack, and host software remain outside the trusted computing base. Bifrost uses FHE as a secure delegation mechanism for projection and feed-forward linear layers on accelerator-backed CKKS, while non-linear operators, attention-side control logic, KV-state transitions, and decrypt-then-encrypt refresh execute inside the CPU TEE.
  Bifrost+ further applies a prefill/decode split: prompt-side KV state is built inside the CPU TEE, and only decode-side state enters the hybrid ciphertext path. In an estimator-style comparison matching Euston's methodology, Bifrost reduces projected latency by 9.25x on GPT-2 (1.5B) and 9.91x on LLaMA 3 (8B). In direct CKKS/FHE deployments, Bifrost+ reduces TTFT by 14.6-45.8x on GPT-2 (124M) and 15.3-53.4x on Qwen3 (0.6B). The systems lesson is selective encrypted execution: use FHE only where ciphertext-only accelerator delegation is required, and keep non-linear, refresh, and prompt-side work inside the CPU TEE.

</details>


### [20] [PARSE: Provenance-Aware Retrieval Sanitization for Professional Domain LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17467)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对提示注入的防御在真实企业文档（如SEC文件、联邦公报、PubMed摘要等）上效果远不如合成基准评测。最强防御Paraphrasing在真实文档上无显著效果（p=0.500），效用从91.8%降至82.8%。本文提出PARSE（基于溯源的检索净化）流水线，通过指令门控路由、句子级注入分类、结构化事实提取与一致性验证循环，在保持86.9%效用的同时将攻击成功率降至15.6%（较基线25.4%降低38%，p=0.014）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入防御主要在合成基准上评测，但这些基准与真实企业文档（更长、更密集、合法权威语言与事实内容交织）存在显著差异，导致防御效果泛化性差。作者旨在揭示这一评测差距，并提出在真实场景中有效的防御方案。

Method: PARSE（Provenance-Aware Retrieval Sanitization）：1) 直接性门控（directiveness gate）将59%低风险文档路由至轻量路径；2) 对高风险文档逐句进行注入似然分类；3) 在改写前提取结构化事实；4) 通过一致性检查循环验证事实保留。构建包含122个任务、5个专业领域（金融、法律、医学、科学、DevOps）的真实文档基准。

Result: PARSE在真实文档上攻击成功率为15.6%，较基线25.4%降低38%；效用为86.9%（接近基线91.8%）。而Paraphrasing在真实文档上无统计显著效果（p=0.500），效用下降至82.8%。PARSE的改进具有统计显著性（p=0.014）且功效充足。

Conclusion: 提示注入防御应在领域匹配的真实文档上评估，而非合成代理基准。PARSE证明了在真实企业场景下兼顾安全与效用的可行性，建议从业者采用基于事实保留的领域感知净化策略。

Abstract: Prompt injection defenses evaluated on synthetic benchmarks do not generalize to real enterprise documents, which are longer, denser, and interleave legitimate authority language with factual content. We demonstrate this gap with a real-document benchmark of 122 tasks across five professional domains (financial, legal, medical, scientific, DevOps) using actual SEC filings, Federal Register rules, PubMed abstracts, arXiv papers, and GitHub postmortems. Paraphrasing, the strongest defense on synthetic benchmarks, shows no statistically significant attack success rate reduction on real documents (p=0.500) while degrading utility from 91.8% to 82.8%. We introduce PARSE (Provenance-Aware Retrieval Sanitization), a domain-aware, fact-preserving sanitization pipeline that classifies each sentence by injection likelihood, extracts structured facts before rewriting, and verifies fact preservation via a consistency-checking loop. A directiveness gate routes 59% of real enterprise documents to a lightweight path, concentrating computational cost on high-risk documents. PARSE achieves 15.6% attack success rate -- a 38% reduction versus the 25.4% baseline -- at 86.9% utility, the only condition that is both statistically significant (p=0.014, adequately powered) and maintains near-baseline utility. Practitioners should evaluate defenses on domain-matched real documents, not synthetic proxies.

</details>


### [21] [SNAS: A Multi-Layer Defense-in-Depth Architecture for Secure Egress in Sandboxed Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17533)
*Niranjan Kumar Sharma,S Muralidhar,Samy Boshra-Riad,Mike Halcrow,Yuxiong He,Nitya Kumar Sharma,Shawn Xia,Haowei Yu,Elliott Brossard,Derek Denny-Brown,Choden Konigsmark,Bhanu Prakash,Brandon Baker,Andong Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Snowpark通过SNAS架构实现安全的外部网络访问，该架构结合eBPF包过滤、GENEVE覆盖网络和分布式出口代理，在保持多租户隔离和资源公平性的同时，支持数据工程和AI/ML工作负载的外部连接需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Snowpark中的数据工程和AI/ML工作负载需要访问外部云API、数据库或特征存储，但在严格多租户隔离和资源公平性要求下，如何提供透明的网络访问成为一个可靠性挑战。

Method: SNAS架构结合了三种核心技术：1) eBPF包过滤，2) GENEVE覆盖网络，3) 分布式出口代理；并实现了基于EDT算法的eBPF带宽限制器、双层级策略执行以及连接限制和端口耗尽防护机制。

Result: SNAS已部署至所有Snowflake区域，支持大规模生产工作负载，包括PB级数据传输和延迟敏感的外部集成。

Conclusion: SNAS在生产环境中成功实现了安全、低开销的策略驱动出口控制，平衡了外部连接需求与多租户隔离及资源公平性要求。

Abstract: Snowpark enables data engineering and AI/ML workloads in Snowflake by executing user-defined functions in secure sandboxes. Many of these workloads require external connectivity to access cloud APIs, external databases, or feature stores, creating a dependability challenge: how to provide transparent network access while preserving strict multi-tenant isolation and resource fairness. This paper presents Secure Network Access in Snowpark (SNAS), a production architecture for secure external communication from sandboxed workloads. SNAS combines Extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) packet filtering, Generic Network Virtualization Encapsulation (GENEVE) overlay networks, and distributed egress proxies for policy-driven egress control with low overhead. We describe the design, deployment, and measured production behavior of SNAS, including an eBPF-based bandwidth limiter using the Earliest Departure Time (EDT) algorithm, dual-tier policy enforcement, and safeguards for connection limiting and port exhaustion. SNAS is deployed across all Snowflake regions and supports large-scale production workloads including petabyte-scale data transfer and latency-sensitive external integrations.

</details>


### [22] [Children Are Not the Enemy: Child-Fit Security as an Alternative to Bans and Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17957)
*Kopo M. Ramokapane,Rui Huan,Zaina Dkaidek,Awais Rashid*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"儿童适配安全"（Child-fit security）设计范式，反对将儿童视为需被排除的攻击者或需被管理的风险，主张技术系统应将儿童视为合法用户，将儿童福祉、发展、隐私、安全、能动性和权利作为核心安全需求，从保护应用、账户和数据转向保护儿童-系统关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有儿童网络安全方法过度依赖"封闭式"机制（禁令、年龄门控、家长控制、监控、屏幕时间限制），将儿童保护简化为限制儿童接触成人系统的问题，这种框架不足以应对儿童数字生活的复杂现实，缺乏作为安全范式的充分性。

Method: 通过概念化"儿童适配安全"范式，与封闭式方法进行对比，定义其核心原则（将儿童视为合法用户、以儿童福祉和发展为核心、保护儿童-系统关系），并讨论其对安全设计的影响，最后提出使其可操作化的研究议程。

Result: 提出了一个以儿童为中心的安全设计新范式，将保护的焦点从应用、账户和数据转移到儿童-系统关系上，同时保护儿童本身及其参与。

Conclusion: "儿童适配安全"范式提供了比现有封闭式方法更全面的安全设计思路，后续需要进一步研究使其可操作化。

Abstract: Digital technologies are now central to children's learning, play, communication, identity formation, and social participation. Yet dominant approaches to children's online safety often rely on containment mechanisms, including bans, age gates, parental controls, monitoring, and screen-time restrictions. These approaches can be useful in specific contexts, but they often frame child protection primarily as a problem of restricting access to systems designed for adults. In this paper, we argue that this framing is inadequate for children's digital lives and insufficient as a security paradigm. We propose Child-fit security, a design paradigm in which technologies likely to be used by children treat a child as legitimate users, not attackers to be excluded, vulnerabilities to be patched, or risks to be managed. In this paradigm, children's wellbeing, development, privacy, safety, agency, and rights become core security requirements. This shifts the focus of protection from apps, accounts, and data to the child-system relationship, which means protecting both the child and their participation. We conceptualise child-fit security, contrast it with containment-oriented approaches, define its core principles, and discuss its implications for security design. We conclude by presenting a research agenda for making child-fit security operational.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Native Success: Auditing Deployment-Interface Exposure of CLIP Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17815)
*Kunlan Xiang,Haomiao Yang,Wenbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFE框架，系统评估后门CLIP模型在不同部署接口（特征提取、检索、重排序、选择）下的风险暴露情况，发现现有后门攻击存在接口间可迁移性不足的问题，并据此提出BadTextTower攻击方法，实现文本侧强暴露而视觉侧干净的后门注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP后门攻击仅在单一任务上验证效果，当模型通过不同部署接口（如检索、重排序、选择等）复用时，后门是否仍然有效尚不清楚，缺乏系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出DIFE（Deployment-Interface Footprint Evaluation）框架，通过指定各组件的读取方式、触发通道、目标事件、参考条件和指标来统一评估不同接口下的后门暴露程度，并引入有效足迹诊断识别携带风险的可复用组件。

Result: 使用DIFE审计现有CLIP后门发现：原生攻击成功不等于检查点级风险认证；暴露遵循组件足迹；文本侧投毒不保证文本编码器可控；部分耦合攻击受机制限制。存在文本编码器本身成为可复用对抗行为载体的问题。

Conclusion: 现有CLIP后门存在重要缺口，即缺乏将文本编码器本身变为可复用对抗行为载体的攻击。提出BadTextTower填补这一空白，该攻击在文本条件检索、重排序和选择接口上产生强暴露，同时保持视觉-only复用几乎干净。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training models are widely reused across downstream interfaces, including feature extraction, retrieval, reranking, and selection. Existing CLIP backdoor, however, usually validate attacks on a small attack-native task, leaving unclear whether the same poisoned checkpoint remains exposed, weakens, or becomes not applicable when reused through other interfaces. We introduce DIFE, a Deployment-Interface Footprint Evaluation framework that audits backdoored CLIP checkpoints across deployment interfaces. DIFE makes various evaluations comparable by specifying each interface's component readout, trigger channel, target event, reference condition, and metric. DIFE also introduces effective-footprint diagnosis to identify the reusable CLIP component or component combination that carries exposure and explains where risk transfers. Auditing reproduced CLIP backdoors with DIFE reveals a structured landscape: native success is not a checkpoint-level risk certificate, exposure follows component footprints, text-side poisoning does not yield textual-encoder control, and some coupled attacks remain mechanism-bound. This audit reveals a import gapin existing CLIP backdoors: a textual encoder that itself becomes a reusable carrier of adversarial behavior. We therefore introduce BadTextTower to fill this gap. BadTextTower produces strong text-conditioned retrieval, reranking, and selection exposure while leaving visual-only reuse nearly clean.

</details>


### [24] [ShellGames: Speculative LLM-Driven SSH Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17986)
*Umberto Salviati,Fabio De Gaspari,Mauro Conti,Luigi Vincenzo Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ShellGames，一种基于LLM的SSH shell模拟器，通过五种互补技术（CoT/少样本学习、内存管理、推测执行、沙箱路由、子版本检测）解决LLM在欺骗系统中缺乏持久状态、输出不一致、幻觉、延迟和易被颠覆等局限。在标准化基准测试中，ShellGames在正确性(89.8%)、一致性(91.8%)、状态跟踪(98%)和鲁棒性(95%)上均显著优于基线，用户研究证实其真实感接近真实shell。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络欺骗和移动目标防御虽然能通过增加不确定性来干扰攻击者，但维持长期、可信的交互式会话仍是一个开放挑战。LLM虽为动态欺骗系统提供可能，但其固有局限（缺乏持久状态、输出不一致、幻觉、延迟、易被行为颠覆）严重限制了其在欺骗场景中的实际应用。

Method: ShellGames采用五种互补技术：(i)自动链式思维(CoT)和少样本学习提升正确性；(ii)内存管理维护系统状态一致性；(iii)推测性命令执行降低响应延迟；(iv)复杂交互命令智能路由至沙箱环境；(v)利用shell环境受限的输入输出域进行子版本检测。同时引入标准化基准测试协议和数据集，涵盖正确性、一致性、状态跟踪和鲁棒性任务。

Result: ShellGames在正确性任务上达到0.898命令准确率（超基线5.3个百分点），一致性上0.918序列级准确率（超36个百分点），状态跟踪上0.98准确率（超18.3个百分点），鲁棒性上0.95准确率（超37个百分点）。20人用户研究表明其真实感接近真实shell，且在感知命令覆盖率上优于传统蜜罐。

Conclusion: ShellGames有效克服了LLM在欺骗系统中的关键局限，通过多种技术协同显著提升了模拟shell的正确性、一致性、状态跟踪能力和鲁棒性，为构建更逼真、可持续的交互式欺骗系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cyber deception and Moving Target Defense are promising strategies that aim to disrupt adversaries by increasing uncertainty. However, sustaining long-lived, credible interactive sessions with adversaries remains an open challenge. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising path toward more dynamic deception systems, but suffer from key limitations that fundamentally limit their applicability, including: lack of persistent state, output inconsistencies, hallucinations, latency, and susceptibility to behavioral subversion that may reveal the deception.
  We propose ShellGames, an SSH shell simulator based on LLM designed to address these limitations. ShellGames combines five complementary techniques: (i) Automatic Chain-of-Thought and few-shot learning to improve correctness; (ii) memory management to maintain system state coherency; (iii) speculative command execution to reduce response latency; (iv) smart routing of complex interactive commands to a sandboxed environment; and (v) subversion detection leveraging the constrained input-output domain of shell environments. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce a standardized benchmarking protocol and dataset spanning correctness, consistency, state tracking, and robustness tasks. ShellGames achieves $0.898$ command accuracy on correctness ($+5.3pp$ over baselines), $0.918$ sequence-level accuracy on consistency ($+36pp$), $0.98$ state tracking accuracy ($+18.3pp$), and $0.95$ accuracy on robustness ($+37pp$). A user study with $n=20$ participants confirms that ShellGames achieves realism comparable to a real shell under free exploration and outperforms traditional honeypots on perceived command coverage.

</details>


### [25] [An Empirical Analysis of AI Slop in Music Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18052)
*Stanley Wu,Josephine Passananti,Viresh Mittal,Wenxin Ding,Haitao Zheng,Ben Y. Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究AI音乐"垃圾内容"（AI slop）是否会在音乐行业形成自给自足的灰色产业链。通过对Spotify数据分析、自行发布AI音乐实验及检测方法评估，发现93%的AI音乐几乎无人收听，发行商对AI音乐政策执行不力，检测方法缺乏准确性。研究表明，随着生成成本下降，除非行业采取具体措施，AI音乐垃圾内容将实现自我维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI音乐垃圾内容（AI slop）是否可能像传统电子邮件垃圾网络一样，发展成一个自给自足的灰色产业，并在其形成规模化之前寻找预防措施。

Method: 1) 分析Spotify上AI音乐的分布、增长与用户参与度数据；2) 通过11家独立音乐发行商自行生成并发布AI音乐，测试发行政策执行情况；3) 评估现有AI音乐检测方法的准确性和鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 93%的AI音乐在Spotify上几乎无播放量且不被推荐，AI音乐人采用"喷洒并祈祷"策略大量发布跨流派作品；2) 各发行商对AI音乐政策不一致且执行不力，发布成本极低；3) 现有AI音乐检测方法缺乏准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 随着AI音乐生成成本持续下降，若音乐行业不采取具体干预措施，AI音乐垃圾内容将发展成自给自足的灰色产业。论文基于研究发现讨论了潜在缓解方法。

Abstract: Generative AI models lower the bar for content creation, making it easy for any user to create professional-looking images, text and music with minimal effort. This has enabled a new cottage industry around creation of "AI slop" mass quantities of mediocre content produced to generate revenue, often through misrepresentation as human-authored content, or scams involving automated scripts and fake consumption.
  While there are obvious parallels between the AI-slop industry and "traditional" email spam networks, it might be too early to determine if AI slop generation can grow into a similar self-sustaining industry. In this paper, we look specifically at the music industry, and explore the question: Can we prevent AI music slop from growing into a self-sustaining shadow industry?
  To answer this question, we characterize the current state of AI slop in music, and its pipeline from generation, distribution, and consumption by users on streaming platforms. By examining growth and engagement on Spotify, we confirm that AI music exhibits AI slop characteristics: the overwhelming majority (93%) of AI music receive few, if any listener plays, and are rarely recommended. AI musicians "spray and pray," releasing large volumes of music across multiple genres in hopes of generating a hit. We also explore the AI slop pipeline by generating and publishing our own AI tracks onto streaming through 11 indie music distributors. We find distributors have inconsistent and largely unenforced policies on AI music, making it surprisingly easy to publish mass produced AI songs. Finally, we consider AI music detection, and find that current methods lack accuracy or robustness. As generation costs decrease, we believe slop generation in music will become self-sustainable, unless concrete steps are taken by the music industry. We consider and discuss potential mitigation methods based on our findings.

</details>


### [26] [Structural Role Injection in Handlebars-Templated LLM Prompts: Triple-Brace Interpolation, Delimiter Family, and the Limits of HTML Auto-Escaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18120)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Handlebars模板引擎的双花括号{x}（HTML转义）和三花括号{x}（原始插入）对结构化角色注入攻击的影响。实验发现，HTML转义仅能覆盖带尖括号的角色分隔符（如ChatML、Llama-3、XML），对Llama-2 [INST]、Human:/Assistant:、Markdown ###等分隔符完全无效。5760次试验表明，GPT-3.5 Turbo在原始插入中97%被劫持，转义后仍有91%；Claude Haiku 4.5几乎完全抵抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Handlebars作为广泛使用的模板引擎（如Microsoft Semantic Kernel默认模板格式），其双花括号{x}被文档标注为安全默认值但仅做HTML转义。研究旨在揭示这种转义选择是否以及如何影响应用对结构化角色注入攻击的暴露程度，以及其能否真正保障安全。

Method: 进行无模型分析建立机制（检查HTML转义对各角色分隔符字符集的改写情况），然后进行5760次试验，覆盖7种分隔符族、2种攻击目标（任务劫持、秘密泄露）、4种模型（GPT-3.5 Turbo、GPT-4o mini、GPT-4.1 mini、Claude Haiku 4.5），总API成本1.63美元。

Result: HTML转义重写尖括号但不重写方括号、冒号或Markdown哈希，因此对ChatML、Llama-3、XML分隔符生存率0.00，对Llama-2 [INST]、Human:/Assistant:、Markdown ###生存率1.00。GPT-3.5 Turbo在原始插入中任务劫持成功率达97%，转义后仍有91%；秘密泄露目标不饱和，更清晰暴露了家族交互。Claude Haiku 4.5几乎完全抵抗两种攻击。

Conclusion: Handlebars的默认HTML转义保护仅限于其字符覆盖的分隔符方案，对其他分隔符无任何保护，无法替代指令与数据的结构性分离。

Abstract: Large language model applications build prompts from templates, and Handlebars is a widely used templating engine and the default prompt-template format in Microsoft Semantic Kernel. Its double-brace {x} expression HTML-escapes the interpolated value and is documented as the safe default; its triple-brace {x} expression inserts the value raw. We show that this choice silently governs an application's exposure to structural role injection, where attacker-controlled data carries chat role delimiters that forge a higher-privilege turn. A model-free analysis establishes the mechanism: Handlebars escaping rewrites angle brackets but not square brackets, colons, or Markdown hashes, so it neutralises ChatML, Llama-3, and XML role delimiters (survival rate 0.00) while leaving Llama-2 [INST], legacy Human:/Assistant:, and Markdown ### delimiters intact (survival rate 1.00 for the last two). We then run 5760 trials across seven delimiter families, two attack objectives, and four models (GPT-3.5 Turbo, GPT-4o mini, GPT-4.1 mini, Claude Haiku 4.5) at a combined API cost of 1.63 USD. GPT-3.5 Turbo follows the task-hijack instruction in 97% of raw and 91% of escaped trials, with the escaping protection concentrated in the angle-bracket families and absent for the colon- and Markdown-based families; the harder secret-exfiltration objective, which does not saturate, exposes the same family interaction more cleanly. Claude Haiku 4.5 resists both objectives almost entirely. The escaped default protects only the delimiter schemes whose characters HTML escaping happens to cover, gives no protection for the rest, and cannot substitute for a structural separation of instruction and data.

</details>


### [27] [Evaluating Open-Source LLMs for Multi-Label ATT&CK Technique Classification on CTI Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18166)
*Ahmed Ryan,Saad Sakib Noor,Md Erfan,Shaswata Mitra,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含2,076条人工标注句子的复杂非结构化CTI数据集，评估了7个开源LLM（8B-236B参数）的ATT&CK多标签分类性能，最高F1仅0.22，表明当前开源LLM尚不满足生产级要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多使用简化的单技术句子，忽略真实CTI报告的复杂性，导致性能结果虚高；开源LLM在复杂非结构化CTI报告上的基线性能尚未被评估。

Method: 构建了包含2,076条人工标注句子（1,281条正例、795条负例）的ground-truth数据集，映射到114个ATT&CK技术（六阶段标注流程，κ=0.68）；评估7个开源LLM（8B-236B参数），测试不同提示策略和温度配置。

Result: 最高F1仅0.22；参数量与F1显著正相关；提示策略和温度无统计显著增益。

Conclusion: 当前开源LLM无法满足生产级ATT&CK分类需求；数据集、基准和发现为未来CTI研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Classifying Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) using MITRE Adversarial Tactics, Techniques, and Common Knowledge (ATT&CK) is essential for proactive defense, but historically required extensive human effort. Pre-Large Language Model (LLM) automation sped up this process, but could not resolve the complex language and multi-step attack patterns found in unstructured CTI reports. LLMs addressed previous limitations by using contextual reasoning to understand unstructured text. However, current evaluations rely on simplified, single-technique sentences that ignore the complexity of real-world CTI reports, which often leads to inflated performance results. Consequently, the baseline performance of open-source LLMs on complex unstructured CTI reports remains unevaluated. To address this gap, we constructed a ground-truth dataset of 2,076 human-annotated sentences (1,281 technique-positive, 795 negative) from 83 complex unstructured CTI reports. These sentences were mapped to 114 unique ATT&CK techniques using a six-phase annotation process, achieving \k{appa} = 0.68 inter-annotator agreement. Using this dataset, we evaluated seven open-source LLMs ranging from 8B to 236B parameters across prompt strategy and temperature configurations. The highest-performing LLM achieved a micro-averaged F1 score of 0.22, establishing the empirical baseline for multi-label ATT&CK classification on complex unstructured CTI. Parameter size showed a statistically significant positive correlation with F1 score. Prompt strategy and temperature produced no statistically significant gains across model configurations. These results indicate that current open-source LLMs are insufficient for production-grade ATT&CK classification. The dataset, benchmark, and findings provide a reproducible foundation for future CTI research.

</details>


### [28] [Multi-Source Cybersecurity Logs: An ATT&CK-Labeled Dataset and SLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18190)
*Abir Ashab Niloy,Ahmed Ryan,Imamul Hossain Rafi,Md Erfan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含系统、网络和浏览器三源日志的多阶段网络攻击数据集（870个会话，约230万事件），并标注了MITRE ATT&CK技术标签（12个战术、53个技术）。通过微调三个小语言模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-3B、Phi-4-Mini），在分块分类任务上准确率从约8%提升至90%-97%，技术识别精确匹配准确率达42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集存在缺陷：网络数据集（如CICIDS、UNSW-NB15）缺少主机和浏览器活动；主机数据集（如LMDG、CICAPT-IIoT）缺乏浏览器遥测；ATLAS数据集虽包含三源但仅标注恶意/良性，缺少ATT&CK技术粒度。目前没有公开数据集同时包含三源日志且具有逐条ATT&CK技术标签，这限制了跨源攻击检测的机器学习研究。

Method: 1) 构建多源日志数据集：在Windows端点上同步采集系统、网络和浏览器活动日志，包含870个会话（70个攻击会话、800个良性会话），约230万事件；2) 标注：使用真实工具（RAT、C2隧道、云外泄等）生成攻击数据，为恶意事件标注ATT&CK技术ID，覆盖12个战术和53个技术；3) 实验验证：使用LoRA微调三个小语言模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-3B、Phi-4-Mini），在分块分类和ATT&CK技术识别两个任务上评估性能。

Result: 微调后所有模型在所有指标上均有提升。分块分类准确率从基线的约8%提升至90%-97%。技术识别任务中，最佳精确匹配准确率为42%，但高部分匹配分数表明模型已掌握大部分潜在推理。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了多源日志ATT&CK标注数据的空白，小语言模型微调后能有效学习跨源攻击模式，但ATT&CK技术精确识别仍具挑战性，为后续研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Multi-stage cyberattacks span system, network, and browser logs. Detecting them requires correlating events across all three sources. Machine learning methods can learn these cross-source patterns, but they need labeled multi-source data. Existing public datasets fall short. Network-only datasets such as CICIDS and UNSW-NB15 miss host and browser activity. Host-focused datasets such as LMDG and CICAPT-IIoT lack browser telemetry. ATLAS includes all three sources but labels events only as malicious or benign, without MITRE Adversarial Tactics, Techniques, and Common Knowledge (ATT&CK) technique granularity. No public dataset combines all three sources with per-entry ATT&CK technique labels. We close the gap by building a multi-source log dataset of 870 sessions (70 attack, 800 benign) and approximately 2.3 million events. We captured system, network, and browser activity simultaneously on Windows endpoints. We labeled malicious events with ATT&CK technique IDs, covering 12 tactics and 53 techniques. We generated all attack data using real tools, including Remote Access Trojan (RAT), Command and Control (C2) tunnels, and cloud exfiltration. To demonstrate learnability, we fine-tuned three Small Language Models (SLMs) (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Llama-3.2-3B, Phi-4-Mini) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We compared each against its base variant across ten metrics on two tasks: chunk classification and ATT&CK technique identification. Fine-tuning improved every model on every metric. Chunk classification accuracy rose from approximately 8% in the base variants to between 90% and 97% after fine-tuning. Technique identification remained challenging, with the best exact-match accuracy at 42%, although high partial-match scores show the models captured most of the underlying reasoning.

</details>


### [29] [A Red-Team Study of Anthropic Fable 5 & Opus 4.8 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18193)
*Nicola Franco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了Anthropic两个前沿大语言模型（Fable 5和Opus 4.8）面对四种自动化越狱攻击的对抗鲁棒性，发现尽管模型能抵抗大多数攻击，但在自适应迭代攻击下仍存在可被利用的脆弱性，且这些漏洞可通过低成本自动化方式稳定复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证前沿大语言模型在对抗性攻击下的实际鲁棒性，检验当前最先进的防御配置是否足以抵御自动化越狱攻击。

Method: 使用HackAgent红队框架，生成数十万次对抗性尝试，覆盖四种自动化越狱攻击家族和十个危害类别的7,826个有害意图。所有明显成功的攻击由三个评审模型组成的评审团进行独立重新裁决（多数投票）。

Result: Opus 4.8在最强的自适应搜索（tree-of-attacks）下有11.5%的意图被攻破，Fable 5为6.1%。两个模型分别产生了1,620个（Opus 4.8）和702个（Fable 5）经评审团确认的有害输出，覆盖所有危害类别，且这些攻击是自动化、低成本、无需人类专家参与的。

Conclusion: 即使是最优秀、经过最充分测试的前沿模型，在持续的自动化压力下仍然可以被可靠攻破，聚合胜率指标不应被视为安全的保证。

Abstract: We evaluate the adversarial robustness of two frontier large language models (LLMs) developed by Anthropic, Fable 5 and Opus 4.8, against four families of automated jailbreak attack across 7 826 harmful intents spanning a ten-category harm taxonomy. Using the HackAgent red-teaming framework, hundreds of thousands of adversarial attempts were generated and every apparent success was independently re-adjudicated by a panel of three judge models (majority vote). Both models resist the majority of attacks, but the residual surface is larger than aggregate framing suggests: it is dominated by adaptive iterative attacks, while static obfuscation is near-fully neutralised. The strongest adaptive search (tree-of-attacks) breaks Opus 4.8 on 11.5% of intents overall, whereas Fable 5 stays in the single digits (6.1% worst-case). Aggregate rates therefore should not be read as reassurance. Even in these hardened configurations, the two models produced 1 620 (Opus 4.8) and 702 (Fable 5) panel-confirmed harmful completions spanning every harm category, located automatically, cheaply, and within the first one or two refinement steps by an attacker model with no human expert in the loop. The reasonable conclusion is that even the best, most-tested frontier models remain reliably breakable under sustained automated pressure.

</details>


### [30] [Seeing Is Not Screening: Multimodal Hidden Instruction Attacks on Agent Skill Scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18198)
*Xiaojun Jia,Jie Liao,Simeng Qin,Ke Ma,Wenbo Guo,Yebo Feng,Aishan Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现现有LLM技能扫描器主要依赖文本信号，忽视图像中隐藏的恶意指令，提出SkillCamo攻击（通过图像隐藏恶意指令）和ExecScan防御（执行驱动的多模态扫描），实验证明图像隐蔽指令可绕过现有检测，而ExecScan能有效提升扫描性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要依赖文本描述、清单和源码作为安全分析信号，无法充分检测视觉传达的恶意意图，导致多模态代理在部署时可能从图像中恢复隐藏的有害操作指令，存在实际安全盲点。

Method: 提出SkillCamo攻击：将恶意指令隐藏在技能配套的图像中，重写周围文档使其自然引用这些图像作为正常流程的一部分，依赖文本引导与视觉负载的联合解释。提出ExecScan防御：执行驱动多模态扫描模块，进行意图提取、行为重建、滥用评估和推理性执行模拟，联合分析文档、代码、引用资源和视觉内容以恢复隐藏指令。

Result: 实验表明图像隐藏的恶意指令能挑战现有技能扫描器，ExecScan可改善技能扫描性能。

Conclusion: 图像可作为多模态LLM系统的隐蔽攻击向量，现有的基于文本的技能扫描存在盲区，执行驱动的多模态扫描是有效的防御方向。

Abstract: Agent skills are emerging as an important attack surface in LLM-based systems. Through an empirical study of existing skill scanners, we find that current defenses primarily rely on textual descriptions, manifests, and source code as the main signals for security analysis, which can leave visually conveyed malicious intent insufficiently examined. This creates a practical blind spot: harmful operational instructions hidden in images may bypass scanning while still being recoverable by multimodal agents during deployment. To systematically investigate this threat, we propose SkillCamo, a document-mediated multimodal instruction attack that conceals malicious instructions within images bundled with a skill while rewriting the surrounding documentation to naturally reference those images as part of the normal workflow. Thus, the attack does not rely on the image alone, but on the joint interpretation of textual guidance and visual payload at execution time. To defend against such attacks, we further propose ExecScan, an execution-grounded multimodal scanning module that performs intent extraction, behavior reconstruction, abuse assessment, and deliberative execution simulation over skill artifacts. ExecScan jointly analyzes documentation, code, referenced resources, and visual content to recover hidden instructions, reconstruct executable behavior chains, and identify downstream risks such as exfiltration, destruction, persistence, deception, and privilege escalation. Extensive experiments show that image-hidden malicious instructions challenge existing skill scanners, while ExecScan can improve the skill scanning performance.

</details>


### [31] [Gatling: Rapid-Fire Consensus from Parallel Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18220)
*Giulia Scaffino,Max Resnick,Joachim Neu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Gatling是一种通过并行运行多个原子广播协议实例并交错其提案调度，以实现任意小（可低于网络延迟）的提案间隔时间的高效共识协议，同时保持正确性和可预测有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识协议在无故障情况下已实现最优的确认延迟下界，但提案间隔时间能否进一步降低至低于一个网络延迟仍是开放问题。Gatling旨在突破这一瓶颈，实现更小的提案间隔时间以提升整体性能。

Method: Gatling并行运行多个黑盒原子广播协议实例，交错安排各实例的提案调度，使得提案生成速度超过传统协议；随后通过确定性交织规则将各实例输出合并为单一全局日志。论文还分析了由崩溃领导者引起的队头阻塞效应，推导出最优并行实例数，并研究了可预测有效性的保持。

Result: 实验证实Gatling可与现成的组件协议配合使用，在不针对最小延迟进行微调的情况下实现低延迟；通过分析得出了最优并行实例数以应对领导者崩溃导致的队头阻塞问题。

Conclusion: Gatling成功将提案间隔时间降低至网络延迟以下，突破了现有共识协议的瓶颈，且可与现成协议兼容使用，为高性能区块链和复制状态机提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Consensus protocols form the core of blockchains and other replicated state machines, ensuring that all correct nodes process the same totally ordered log of input transactions. In fault-free executions, performance is driven by the good-case transaction latency -- the time between a transaction becoming known to all nodes and its confirmation by the consensus protocol -- which depends on both how frequently proposals are made and, once made, how quickly they are confirmed. While prior work has established tight lower bounds on confirmation latency that modern protocols already achieve, it remains open whether the inter-proposal time can be further reduced below the state-of-the-art of one network delay.
  We introduce Gatling, an atomic broadcast protocol that achieves arbitrarily small inter-proposal times under rotating leader schedules; in particular, smaller than the network delay. Gatling runs multiple parallel instances of a black-box atomic broadcast protocol and staggers their proposal schedules to generate proposals in faster succession than state-of-the-art protocols. A deterministic interleaving rule merges the outputs of these instances into a single global log. We analyze the effects of head-of-line blocking caused by crashed leaders, and derive Gatling's optimal number of parallel instances. We further study the impact of Gatling on predictable validity and present two variants that retain this property. Finally, our experiments confirm that Gatling can be used with off-the-shelf component protocols to achieve low latency without fine-tuning the component protocol for minimum latency.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [32] [Performance-Driven Environment Abstraction with Multi-Timescale Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17377)
*Yue Guan,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向性能驱动的环境抽象方法，通过状态聚合和动作共享来优化大规模MDP中的决策质量，并开发了多时间尺度强化学习框架，可联合调整策略和树结构抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境抽象方法多关注几何或拓扑结构保留，而非直接优化决策质量，导致抽象结果与任务目标脱节。

Method: 将抽象建模为受控近似：聚合状态空间并在每个聚合状态内强制执行共享动作分布；推导了分离值函数近似误差和动作共享损失的性能保证；基于此分析，设计多时间尺度强化学习框架，根据Q值差异自适应细化或粗化状态空间区域。

Result: 与actor-critic基线相比，显著压缩状态空间、提升样本效率、加快重规划速度。

Conclusion: 直接优化决策质量的抽象方法能够在保持性能的前提下实现大规模状态压缩，多时间尺度联合优化策略与抽象结构是有效的。

Abstract: We study performance-driven environment abstraction for decision-making in large Markov decision processes. Rather than preserving geometric or topological structure, we seek abstractions that directly optimize decision quality. We model abstraction as a controlled approximation obtained by aggregating the state space and enforcing a shared action distribution within each aggregated state. For a fixed partition, we establish a performance guarantee that separates value-function approximation error from the loss introduced by action sharing. Guided by this analysis, we develop a multi-timescale reinforcement learning framework that jointly adapts the policy and a tree-structured environment abstraction. The resulting algorithm refines and coarsens regions of the state space based on Q-value discrepancies, balancing performance against abstraction size and complexity. Empirical results demonstrate substantial state compression, improved sample efficiency, and faster replanning compared to actor-critic baselines.

</details>


### [33] [Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17465)
*Shiqi Zhang,Wuwei Wu,Jaemin Oh,Jie Chen,Xiaoning Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Perron-Frobenius算子匹配（PFOM）生成框架，通过积分PF算子匹配密度演化，统一了流、扩散和跳跃模型。证明在Bregman散度中仅KL散度保持密度级与样本条件目标等价，得到等价于Koopman路径匹配的实用损失。进一步开发Nesterov加速训练与采样方法，稳定离散化并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流、扩散、跳跃模型）在框架上各自独立，缺乏统一的算子理论视角。同时，密度演化匹配的损失函数设计在理论等价性上尚未充分探索，需要建立更严谨的理论基础。

Method: 1) 提出PFOM框架，利用Perron-Frobenius积分算子统一描述不同生成模型的密度演化过程；2) 证明在Bregman散度族中仅KL散度能保证密度级目标与样本条件目标等价，推导出与Koopman路径匹配等价的实用损失；3) 引入Nesterov加速技术优化训练和采样过程，改善离散化稳定性并加速收敛。

Result: 在高斯混合和双月数据集上，PFOM在KL散度、Wasserstein-2距离和最大均值差异（MMD）指标上实现更快的下降速度，并在壁钟时间效率上得到改善（经实验验证）。

Conclusion: PFOM统一了算子理论识别与现代生成建模，为自适应字典和高维应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Perron--Frobenius Operator Matching (PFOM), a generative framework that matches density evolution via the integral PF operator, subsuming flow, diffusion, and jump models. We prove that among Bregman divergences, only Kullback--Leibler divergence preserves equality between density-level and sample-conditioned objectives, yielding a practical loss equivalent to Koopman path matching. We further develop Nesterov-accelerated training and sampling that stabilize discretization and accelerate convergence. %On Gaussian mixtures and two-moons, PFOM achieves faster KL/$W_2$/MMD decrease and improved wall-clock efficiency with empirical validation. PFOM unifies operator-theoretic identification with modern generative modeling and opens paths to adaptive dictionaries and high-dimensional applications.

</details>


### [34] [ReRAM-aware Model Finetuning addressing I-V Non-linearity and Retention Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17471)
*Ching-Yi Lin,Shamik Kundu,Arnab Raha,Sahil Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微调的硬件感知训练算法，通过范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性，并将保留误差融入正则化损失，在ReRAM上实现鲁棒的DNN部署，仅需极少的训练开销，在多个大规模模型和任务上取得了接近基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPU/GPU/NPU受限于冯·诺依曼瓶颈，ReRAM-based IMC虽有高密度、低能耗优势，但实际部署受限于非理想特性；现有硬件感知训练框架往往需要从头训练，对于现代大规模模型计算代价过高。

Method: 提出基于微调的硬件感知训练算法：1）采用范围收缩的sinh变换缓解I-V非线性；2）将保留误差（retention errors）直接纳入微调过程中的正则化损失函数。

Result: 在ResNet18和DeiT-Tiny上达到与基线模型相近的精度；MobileNetV3系列在ImageNet上精度下降小于2%；SQuAD v2数据集上F-1分数仅下降1个点。

Conclusion: 所提微调方法能以极小训练开销实现ReRAM上DNN的鲁棒部署，有效缓解器件非理想性带来的精度损失，适用于图像分类和问答等多种任务。

Abstract: Traditional CPU, GPU, and NPU architectures are increasingly limited by the von Neumann bottleneck. While In-Memory Computing (IMC) using ReRAM crossbar arrays offers a high-density, energy-efficient alternative, its practical deployment is constrained through their non-idealities. Existing hardware-aware training frameworks often require training from scratch, which is computationally prohibitive for modern large-scale models. In this work, we propose a finetuning-based hardware-aware training algorithm that enables robust DNN deployment on ReRAM with minimal training overhead. Our approach mitigates I-V non-linearity by applying a range-shrunk sinh transformation and incorporates retention errors directly into a regularization loss during the finetuning process. We evaluate our framework across models and tasks such as image classification and question-answering (QA). Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves similar accuracy on large-scale models like ResNet18 and DeiT-Tiny as the base model. In-case of ImageNet for MobileNetV3 families the technique has only less than 2% accuracy degradation. Further, applying the technique on the SQuAD v2 dataset results in only 1 point degradation of F-1 score.

</details>


### [35] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前多模态大模型的知识编辑存在“编辑解耦失败”问题：当模型通过多模态输入更新知识后，单模态触发下仍会回退到旧知识。论文发现实体知识在MLLM中并非统一存储，而是分散在不同模态的解耦路径中。为此提出DECODE方法，显式解耦并定位特定知识的模态神经元组，实现跨模态触发下的有效知识更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并解决多模态大模型知识编辑中一个被忽视的关键问题：编辑解耦失败——即多模态输入下更新的知识在单模态输入时失效，退回编辑前的旧知识。

Method: 提出DECODE方法，通过显式解耦并定位特定知识对应的模态特异性神经元组，使知识更新能有效跨模态传播。

Result: 大量实验表明DECODE能够在不同模态触发条件下实现一致的知识更新，有效缓解了编辑解耦失败问题。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型中的实体知识以解耦的模态特异性路径存储，而非统一表征；DECODE通过定位和更新这些模态特异性神经元组，实现了跨模态触发器下的一致性知识编辑。
