Abstract: Looped Transformers scale latent computation by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but sequential looping increases latency and KV-cache memory with the loop count. Parallel loop Transformers (PLT) alleviate this cost through cross-loop position offsets (CLP) and shared-KV gated sliding-window attention, making loop count a practical design choice. We therefore study PLT loop-count selection through a gain--cost view: an extra loop may refine representations, but CLP also introduces a positional mismatch at each loop boundary. We instantiate this study by training LoopCoder-v2, a family of 7B PLT coders with different loop counts, from scratch on 18T tokens, followed by matched instruction tuning and evaluation. Empirically, the two-loop variant delivers broad gains over the non-looped baseline across code generation, code reasoning, agentic software engineering, and tool-use benchmarks, improving SWE-bench Verified from 43.0 to 64.4 points and Multi-SWE from 14.0 to 31.0 points. In contrast, variants with three or more loops regress, revealing a strongly non-monotonic loop-count effect. Our diagnostics show that loop 2 provides the main productive refinement, while later loops yield diminishing, oscillatory updates and reduced representational diversity. Because the CLP-induced mismatch remains roughly fixed as refinement gains shrink, the offset cost increasingly dominates. This gain--cost trade-off explains PLT's saturation at two loops and provides diagnostics for loop-count selection.

</details>


### [112] [Catastrophic Forgetting is Low-Rank: A Function-Space Theory for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18024)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在神经正切核(NTK)框架下给出了持续学习中灾难性遗忘的函数空间解释：新任务训练通过跨任务核导致旧任务预测漂移，推导出遗忘向量的闭式解，并揭示遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模式中，为设计针对性光谱正则化器提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习研究多从参数漂移、重放或蒸馏角度分析灾难性遗忘，但无法识别哪些输出空间方向是脆弱的，缺乏函数空间层面的理解。

Method: 在NTK机制下推导出新任务训练导致旧任务预测漂移的闭式解（通过跨任务核），在冻结骨干+线性头的PEFT-CL中该预测精确成立，非线性适配器或全微调时则为局部NTK近似。进而揭示遗忘与旧任务NTK本征模式的关系，并给出克罗内克秩缩放规则。

Result: 推导出遗忘向量的闭式预测器；发现遗忘集中在少量旧任务NTK本征模式中；给出冻结线性头下的克罗内克秩缩放规则；阐明了与先前NTK重叠理论的关系。

Conclusion: 参数空间正则化器可能无法捕捉输出空间干扰，本文的函数空间视角和闭式解为设计靶向光谱正则化器提供了动机和理论基础。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual adaptation is usually studied through parameter drift, replay, or distillation, but these views do not identify which output-space directions are vulnerable. We give a function-space account in the NTK regime: new-task training induces old-task prediction drift through the cross-task kernel, yielding a closed-form predictor for the forgetting vector before any new-task gradient step. In frozen-backbone linear-head PEFT-CL, where the model is linear in the trainable parameters, the predictor is exact up to numerical precision; for nonlinear adapters/full fine-tuning, it is a local NTK approximation. The same expression reveals that forgetting concentrates in a small number of old-task NTK eigenmodes and under frozen linear heads gives a Kronecker scaling rule for the vulnerable rank. These results clarify the relation to prior NTK-overlap theory, explain why parameter-space regularizers can miss output-space interference, and motivate a targeted spectral regularizer.

</details>


### [113] [ConTex: Reformulating Counterfactual Generation For Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18049)
*Jan Voets,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen,Sebastian Baum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTex，一种模型无关的分解架构，通过学习全局一致的干预策略来为时间序列预测生成反事实解释，在保持高有效性的同时实现稀疏干预、约0.007秒的实时推理和12-36倍的计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实例优化的反事实生成方法存在实例间不一致性、高计算成本和有限的实时适用性，无法满足基于深度学习的时序预测决策对可操作洞察的需求。

Method: 将反事实生成重新定义为学习全局一致的干预策略问题，提出ConTex架构，包含时序上下文编码器和条件编码器，以及分别捕获时间相关性和修改强度的两个输出头，通过单次前向传播生成跨时间和特征维度的可解释干预。

Result: 在多个预测架构和基准数据集上实现最先进的有效性，生成最小化必要干预次数的稀疏反事实，计算成本降低12-36倍，支持约0.007秒的实时推理。

Conclusion: ConTex通过全局一致的分解架构有效解决了实例级反事实生成的不稳定性和不一致性问题，在保持高有效性的同时大幅提升计算效率和实时性，适用于实际时间序列预测中的决策支持。

Abstract: Decision-making with deep learning-based time series forecasting requires not only accurate predictions but also actionable insights. However, current architectures do not inherently provide such information. Specifically, guidance is needed on how current conditions must be modified to shift from a predicted outcome to a desired future scenario. Counterfactual explanations provide a natural framework for this task, as they represent minimal input changes that alter the model's prediction, indicating when and how intervention is required. Existing approaches rely on instance-wise optimization, leading to inconsistency across instances, high computational costs, and limited applicability in real-time settings.
  To address these limitations, we reformulate counterfactual generation for time series forecasting as the problem of learning a globally consistent intervention strategy, allowing counterfactuals to be generated through a single shared function. We propose Counterfactual Time Series Explanations (ConTex), a model-agnostic, decomposed architecture comprising a temporal context encoder and a conditional encoder, followed by two heads that capture interventions in terms of temporal relevance and modification strength. This structure overcomes the instability and inconsistency of instance-based approaches by producing targeted, interpretable interventions across time and feature dimensions in a single forward pass, making it suitable for real-time applications.
  Across multiple forecasting architectures and benchmark datasets, ConTex achieves state-of-the-art validity while generating sparse counterfactuals that minimize the number of necessary interventions. Additionally, our approach reduces computational cost by at least 12-36x compared to instance-wise generation and supports real-time inference at approximately 0.007 seconds.

</details>


### [114] [NoiseTilt: Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernels for Diffusion Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18066)
*Jisung Hwang,Yunhong Min,Jaihoon Kim,I-Chao Shen,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声倾斜反向核（NTRK），一种奖励引导的扩散采样方法，通过向噪声项注入奖励梯度，保持预训练反向核不变，仅需每步单次采样，在保持样本质量的同时显著提升奖励对齐性能，计算量降低20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励引导扩散采样方法存在权衡：基于梯度的引导通过偏移反向均值来引导生成，但会将中间状态推离模型训练区域，降低质量；基于搜索的方法保持质量但缺乏梯度信号。尚无方法能同时兼顾两者。

Method: NTRK固定反向均值不变，将噪声项向高奖励方向偏置。引入白化算子作为核心机制，使奖励梯度能够安全地作为噪声注入而不丢失引导信号，同时保持预训练反向核不变，每步仅需单个样本。

Result: 在多种奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在不损失样本质量的情况下优于最近的最先进基线。在美学生成任务上，NTRK仅用25次NFE就超越了最佳基线在500次NFE下的奖励，实现了20倍的计算量降低。

Conclusion: NTRK成功解决了奖励引导扩散采样中质量保持与梯度引导之间的矛盾，通过创新的白化算子实现高效、高质量的奖励对齐生成。

Abstract: We introduce the Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel (NTRK), a reward-guided diffusion sampler that injects reward gradients through the noise term, leaving the pretrained reverse kernel unchanged and requiring only a single sample per step. Reward-guided sampling at inference time has greatly expanded the versatility of pretrained diffusion models. Yet existing methods face a trade-off. Gradient-based guidance shifts the reverse mean, steering generation but pushing intermediate states outside the region that the model was trained on and degrading quality. Search-based methods preserve quality but gain no gradient signal. No prior method achieves both. NTRK resolves this by keeping the reverse mean fixed and biasing the noise term toward high reward. We introduce a whitening operator, the central mechanism behind NTRK, that makes the reward gradient safe to inject as noise without losing its guiding signal. Across various reward alignment tasks, NTRK outperforms recent state-of-the-art baselines without losing sample quality. Remarkably, on aesthetic generation, NTRK surpasses the reward of the best baseline at 500 NFEs using only 25 NFEs, a 20$\times$ reduction in compute.

</details>


### [115] [Volterra Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18071)
*Yusen Jia,Bingyan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Volterra生成模型，一种基于分数核路径依赖噪声的连续时间分数阶扩散模型，通过有限维马尔可夫提升处理非马尔可夫动力学，并设计了稳定化条件与桥接采样器，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分数扩散模型使用布朗运动扰动，其噪声是无记忆的。本文希望引入具有路径依赖性的分数阶噪声（Volterra过程），以生成更持久的扰动结构，提升生成质量。

Method: 1) 将分数核噪声的前向过程通过高斯求积法和混合有限差分指数近似构造有限维马尔可夫提升；2) 推导增广线性高斯前向过程，利用残差状态和解析辅助高斯得分保持学习在数据维度上；3) 识别协方差和逆向时间退化问题，提出稳定化条件；4) 针对刚性较大的提升，设计高斯桥重建采样器。

Result: 在MNIST上，持久性分数扰动配合小马尔可夫提升改善了分数生成效果；在CIFAR-10上提供了有前景的自然图像扩展；桥采样器为大规模提升提供了稳定机制。

Conclusion: Volterra生成模型通过引入路径依赖噪声扩展了扩散模型的框架，理论上有误差界保证，实验验证了其在小规模和大规模图像生成上的有效性及稳定性。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models typically use Brownian perturbations, which provide tractable reverse-time dynamics but impose memoryless noising. We introduce Volterra generative models, a continuous-time score-based framework whose forward process injects path-dependent noise through fractional kernels. To handle the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale dynamics, we construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature in both regimes and a hybrid finite-difference exponential approximation in the smooth regime. We prove squared error bounds, derive an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, and show that the learning can remain data-dimensional by considering residual states and analytic auxiliary Gaussian scores. We also identify covariance and reverse-time degeneracies caused by shared Brownian factors and signed smooth-regime weights. The degeneracy motivates stabilized conditioning and, for stiff larger lifts, a Gaussian-bridge reconstruction sampler. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that persistent fractional perturbations with small Markovian lifts can improve score-based generation on MNIST and provide a promising extension to natural images, while the bridge sampler provides a stability mechanism for larger lifts.

</details>


### [116] [Edge Flow: A Tractable and Predictive Continuous-Time Model for Gradient Descent at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18080)
*Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Edge Flow，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的系统，用于建模深度学习在边缘稳定性（EoS）状态下的梯度下降动力学，该模型将动力学分解为中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度三个分量，通过自稳定反馈回路实现锐度稳定，且离散化仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在边缘稳定性（EoS）状态下，损失Hessian的最大特征值接近稳定性阈值2/η，而经典分析工具（如梯度流和下降引理）在此状态下不适用，因此需要寻找一种适用于EoS的连续时间模型。

Method: 提出Edge Flow，由三个耦合常微分方程组成，将动力学分解为中心（沿对称化损失的修正梯度流）、振荡方向（通过Rayleigh商动力学跟踪Hessian的顶特征向量）和振荡幅度（根据锐度是否超过阈值2/η呈指数增长或衰减）。锐度稳定通过耦合动力学的自稳定反馈回路实现。

Result: Edge Flow能够至少与先前提出的EoS连续时间模型一样忠实地跟踪梯度下降的动力学，并且额外解决了EoS起始阶段锐度的振荡问题，为该状态下的不稳定性理解和缓解提供了原则性框架。

Conclusion: Edge Flow是一个可处理、忠实且具有预测性的EoS梯度下降动力学模型，其离散化计算效率高，为理解和缓解EoS状态下的不稳定性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Gradient descent in deep learning may operate at the edge of stability (EoS), a regime in which the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$, where $η$ is the learning rate. Classical analysis tools such as gradient flow and the descent lemma do not apply here, motivating the search for a continuous-time model valid at EoS. We propose Edge Flow, a system of three coupled ordinary differential equations that provides a tractable, faithful, and predictive model of gradient descent dynamics at EoS. Edge Flow decomposes the dynamics into a center, an oscillation direction, and an oscillation magnitude. The center follows a modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss; the direction tracks a top eigenvector of the Hessian via Rayleigh quotient dynamics; and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially depending on whether the sharpness exceeds or falls below the threshold $2/η$. Crucially, sharpness stabilization emerges from the coupled dynamics via a self-stabilization feedback loop. Discretizing Edge Flow only requires two gradient evaluations and one Hessian--vector product at each iteration. We demonstrate empirically that Edge Flow tracks the dynamics of gradient descent at least as faithfully as previously proposed continuous-time EoS models, while in addition resolving the oscillation of the sharpness at the onset of EoS, and that it provides a principled framework for understanding and mitigating instabilities in this regime.

</details>


### [117] [From Reasoning Traces to Reusable Modules: Understanding Compositional Generalization in Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18089)
*Lingjing Kong,Xin Liu,Guangyi Chen,Martin Q. Ma,Xiangchen Song,Yuekai Sun,Mikhail Yurochkin,Taylor W. Killian,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Kun Zhang,Eric P. Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出后训练中SFT和RL通过组合泛化协同作用的理论框架：SFT提供可复用的原子模块（技能和路由机制），RL通过分解复合轨迹识别这些模块并实现组合泛化。实验表明RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重组以解决新问题，且复合训练优于原子模块单独训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释SFT与RL结合为何能有效将LLM转化为强推理模型，揭示其背后机制是组合泛化。

Method: 提出层次化隐变量选择模型形式化组合泛化；理论分析SFT和RL的非对称互补角色；设计受控实验验证理论预测。

Result: RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重组泛化到新配置；复合轨迹训练优于单独原子模块训练；SFT需覆盖所有原子模块，RL应聚焦SFT未覆盖的新组合。

Conclusion: SFT和RL在后训练中扮演互补角色：SFT提供原子模块材料，RL负责分解和重组以实现组合泛化；最优协议是SFT保证原子模块覆盖，RL探索新组合。

Abstract: Post-training pipelines that combine supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) have emerged as the key recipe for transforming large language models (LLMs) into robust reasoners. We argue that this combined success is driven by compositional generalization, which we formalize through a hierarchical latent selection model. In this framework, reasoning traces are generated by a cascade of discrete latent selection variables corresponding to reusable atomic modules, including both skills (local operations) and routing mechanisms (how intermediate information is selected, reused, and composed). Within this model, we theoretically show that SFT and RL play asymmetric, complementary roles: SFT supplies the raw module materials in compositional traces, and RL decomposes those traces to identify the latent atomic modules and enable compositional generalization. We design controlled experiments to validate this theory. Our results demonstrate that RL can extract atomic modules from compound traces supplied by SFT and recombine them to solve new configurations. Moreover, we find that training on compound traces yields stronger generalization than training on isolated atomic modules. Finally, we investigate the relationship between SFT and RL data and identify an effective protocol in which SFT ensures coverage of all atomic modules through compositional traces, while RL focuses on novel compositions outside the SFT support to drive exploration.

</details>


### [118] [S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18096)
*Marco Deano,Filippo Ziche,Nicola Bombieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对结构化状态空间模型（S4和S4D）的增量式算子级剪枝方法，通过结构化掩码与微调相结合的方式逐步剪枝模型算子，在保持预测性能的同时显著降低推理成本，实验表明最多可剪枝70%的算子而不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管S4和S4D模型在捕捉长程依赖关系方面表现出色，但其计算和内存开销较大，在时间和资源受限的场景下部署困难，目前尚缺乏系统性的结构化算子剪枝研究来提升其效率。

Method: 提出一种增量式、算子级的结构化剪枝方法，通过交替进行结构化掩码和微调来逐步剪枝模型算子，并联合监控准确率和推理延迟，在一个统一的训练与评估框架中实现效率-准确率的系统化权衡探索。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，剪枝高达70%的模型算子在大多数情况下仍能保持原始模型的性能，同时显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 结构化算子剪枝是一种此前未被探索的、有效的SSM效率优化策略，有助于在资源受限的实际场景中部署S4和S4D模型。

Abstract: Structured State Space Models (SSMs), including the S4 and S4D architectures, have recently emerged as powerful alternatives to attention-based models for capturing long-range dependencies in sequential data. Despite their strong empirical performance, deploying these models in time- and resource-constrained settings remains challenging due to their computational and memory demands. In this paper, we propose a novel incremental, operator-level pruning approach for S4- and S4D-based models that significantly reduces inference cost while preserving predictive performance. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically investigate structured operator pruning for SSMs. Our method progressively prunes model operators by interleaving structured masking with fine-tuning, while jointly monitoring accuracy and inference latency. We implement this approach within a unified training and evaluation framework that enables systematic exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that pruning up to 70% of the model operators preserves the performance of the original models in most cases, while substantially reducing inference latency. These results demonstrate that structured operator pruning is an effective and previously unexplored strategy for improving the efficiency of SSMs and facilitate their deployment in practical, resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Minimum Zero-Forcing Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18106)
*Steve Halley,Maurício Gruppi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的SD-ZFS框架，用于在无向图中寻找最小零强制集（一个NP-hard的图着色问题），实验表明该框架在最优解和贪心启发式方法对比中表现有效，并分析了网络结构对问题的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小零强制集问题在网络科学、网络控制和逻辑电路设计等领域有重要应用，且已被证明是NP-hard问题，现有方法难以高效求解，因此需要新的解决方案。

Method: 提出SD-ZFS框架，将S2V-DQN强化学习架构适配到零强制集问题，训练多个模型并在不同结构图数据集上进行性能分析。

Result: 该框架在最优解和贪心启发式方法对比中表现有效；训练的模型具有良好的泛化性、可扩展性和迁移性；网络结构对问题求解有显著影响。

Conclusion: 强化学习框架可以有效解决最小零强制集问题，网络结构是影响求解效果的关键因素，为通过机器学习解决该问题提供了新的思路。

Abstract: This paper explores the problem of finding the minimum zero-forcing set on undirected graphs and proposes an adapted machine-learning framework to solve the problem. The minimum zero-forcing set problem is a graph coloring problem where the color of an initial set of nodes propagates throughout a network. The set of nodes is zero-forcing if it forces all uncolored nodes to change color under the constraint of the color-change rule. There are several applications to this problem across different domains such as network science, network control, and designing logical circuits. Finding the minimum zero-forcing set is shown to be NP-hard. We propose a reinforcement learning framework, SD-ZFS, that adapts the S2V-DQN architecture to the ZFS problem. We train several models on this adapted framework and analyze the performance across graph datasets that have varying structures. We evaluate how the models trained on the framework generalize, scale, and transfer to different network types. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework when compared against the optimal solution and greedy heuristic. We provide further insight into how the ZFS problem can be solved through machine-learning and the influence of network structure on the problem.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Fair Pareto-Optimal Policies in Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18111)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在多策略多目标强化学习（MORL）中形式化公平优化问题，提出三种融合广义基尼福利函数（GGF）的新算法，通过非平稳策略、状态增强及随机策略学习一组确保公平性的帕累托最优策略集，实验证明其优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单策略MORL方法虽能用福利函数（如GGF）学习固定偏好下的公平策略，但无法为动态或未知用户偏好提供多样性策略集。本文旨在解决这一局限性，将公平优化问题扩展到多策略MORL场景。

Method: (1) 理论证明凹分段线性福利函数（如GGF）的公平策略仍属于凸覆盖集（CCS）；(2) 引入非平稳策略（通过累积奖励历史状态增强）和随机策略以动态适应历史不公；(3) 提出三种算法：GGF+多策略MOQL、状态增强多策略MOQL（学习非平稳策略）及其扩展（学习随机策略）。

Result: 在多个测试域中，所提方法能够学习到一组适应不同用户偏好的公平策略集，性能优于现有SOTA MORL基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为多策略MORL中的公平性优化提供了理论基础与有效算法框架，证明了非平稳与随机策略对提升公平性的价值，为动态偏好场景下的公平决策开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Fairness is an important aspect of decision-making in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), where policies must ensure both optimality and equity across multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. While single-policy MORL methods can learn fair policies for fixed user preferences using welfare functions such as the generalized Gini welfare function (GGF), they fail to provide the diverse set of policies necessary for dynamic or unknown user preferences. To address this limitation, we formalize the fair optimization problem in multi-policy MORL, where the goal is to learn a set of Pareto-optimal policies that ensure fairness across all possible user preferences. Our key technical contributions are threefold: (1) We show that for concave, piecewise-linear welfare functions (e.g., GGF), fair policies remain in the convex coverage set (CCS), which is an approximated Pareto front for linear scalarization. (2) We demonstrate that non-stationary policies, augmented with accrued reward histories, and stochastic policies improve fairness by dynamically adapting to historical inequities. (3) We propose three novel algorithms, which include integrating GGF with multi-policy multi-objective Q-Learning (MOQL), state-augmented multi-policy MOQL for learning non-statoinary policies, and its novel extension for learning stochastic policies. We evaluate our algorithms across various domains and compare our methods against the state-of-the-art MORL baselines. The empirical results show that our methods learn a set of fair policies that accommodate different user preferences.

</details>


### [121] [Ternary Mamba: Grouped Quantization-Aware Training of W1.58A16 State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18114)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Sahil Dilip Panse,Swathika N*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预训练权重的分组量化感知训练（QAT）与知识蒸馏方法，仅需1亿token（约4 GPU小时）即可将Mamba-2 1.3B模型压缩3.61倍（至744MB），零样本准确率达48.1%，逼近Bi-Mamba的48.4%。相比从零训练（需1500亿token），边际token预算降低1000倍。同时发现QAT中可学习量化尺度导致的零比例坍缩新问题，并指出Transformer的后处理纠错策略因SSM的递归特性而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三元量化状态空间模型（如Slender-Mamba）需要从零训练1500亿token，训练成本极高，限制了边缘设备部署。本文旨在探索是否可以利用预训练权重进行数据高效的三元量化压缩。

Method: 采用分组量化感知训练（QAT）结合知识蒸馏，以冻结的FP16教师模型指导三元量化学生模型训练；在Mamba-2 1.3B模型上进行实验，仅用1.02亿token微调。

Result: 模型从2,687MB压缩至744MB（3.61倍压缩），零样本7任务平均准确率48.1%，与从零训练的Bi-Mamba（48.4%）差距在±0.9pp置信区间内。训练仅需4 GPU小时（单H100）。

Conclusion: 三元SSM无需昂贵的从零训练；基于预训练权重的QAT+KD是数据高效的替代方案。同时发现SSM特有的零比例坍缩问题，且Transformer的常见后处理纠错策略因递归误差累积而对SSM无效。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba-2 offer linear-time inference but their memory footprint limits edge deployment. Prior ternary SSM work (Slender-Mamba) trains from scratch on 150B tokens; we show a pretrained checkpoint suffices, reducing the marginal token budget by 1,000x. Using grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) with knowledge distillation from a frozen FP16 teacher, we compress Mamba-2 1.3B to 3.61x (2,687 to 744 MB) and achieve 48.1% zero-shot accuracy (7-task average) in just 102M tokens (4 GPU-hours, single H100) -- approaching Bi-Mamba's 48.4% (within +/-0.9pp CI). This QAT-from-pretrained setting reveals zero-ratio collapse, a novel instability caused by learnable quantization scales that does not arise in from-scratch training. We further show that post-hoc correction strategies effective for Transformers fail for SSMs due to error accumulation through the recurrence. These results demonstrate that ternary SSMs do not require expensive from-scratch training: QAT from pretrained checkpoints with KD is a data-efficient alternative.

</details>


### [122] [Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18122)
*Mostafa Darvishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文面向微控制器平台，系统性地阐述了嵌入式机器学习工作流中的关键工程决策，包括采样缓冲、特征提取、不平衡验证、模型/运行时协同设计及流式部署，并通过惯性运动识别和关键词唤醒两个案例验证了设计规则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式机器学习将推理从云端迁移到资源受限设备，但通用机器学习教程往往忽略采样、缓冲、特征提取等工程细节，导致部署效率低下。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供面向微控制器平台的系统性工程实践指南。

Method: 采用系统合成方法，以两个代表性信号家族（惯性运动识别和关键词唤醒）贯穿全文，详细阐述嵌入式ML工作流的各环节：采样与缓冲策略、特征提取作为降维手段、类别不平衡下的验证方法、模型与运行时的协同设计、以及流式部署方案。

Result: 给出了基于实际案例的工程决策方案：惯性运动识别中，将2秒三轴加速度窗口转换为均方根和频谱特征；关键词唤醒中，对音频进行采样、抗混叠滤波、MFCC变换后由紧凑型一维卷积网络处理。最终提炼出包含数据整理、量化、阈值设定、调度和现场监控在内的稳健设备端推理设计规则。

Conclusion: 嵌入式机器学习部署需要关注数据采集到现场监控的全链路工程决策，本文提供的设计规则可指导在微控制器平台上实现鲁棒、高效的设备端推理。

Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

</details>


### [123] [Kolmogorov Regression for Robust Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18186)
*Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于后向Kolmogorov方程将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间的方法，通过确定性边界值PDE问题替代随机分数匹配，利用高斯测度理论和有色噪声分布实现轨迹正则化，在PushT操作基准和CONWIP制造线控制中分别取得了17%的奖励提升和28.4%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维扩散策略因离散化伪影导致时间漂移，降低了长时域部署性能，需要一种能保证轨迹正则性和收敛性的方法。

Method: 引入后向Kolmogorov方程将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间；用有色噪声分布实现扩散噪声协方差算子；提出精度加权的Cameron-Martin损失训练扩散模型；引入Kolmogorov残差作为推理时的PDE诊断工具；结合Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论认证调度策略。

Result: PushT基准：最大回合奖励提升17%（0.95 vs 0.78），步间漂移减少67.6%；CONWIP制造线：RMSE降低28.4%，饥饿事件召回率1.0，瓶颈识别Precision@1=1.0，信噪比13倍；死锁事件减少96%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过确定性PDE框架和谱加权机制，有效解决了扩散策略的时间漂移问题，提供了收敛保证和无需奖励信号的故障检测能力，在机器人操作和制造控制领域均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional (FD) diffusion policies exhibit temporal drift owing to discretization artifacts that degrade long-horizon performance (when deployed on physical systems). We introduce a backward Kolmogorov equation that lifts diffusion policies to a Cameron-Martin space -- a subset of the Hilbert space. Essentially, replacing stochastic score matching with a deterministic boundary-value PDE problem. Our core innovation thrives on Gaussian measure theory whereupon the diffusion noise covariance operator is realized from a colored noise distribution which prescribes a notion of regularity on samples from the model at inference time. We train the diffusion model with a derived precision-weighted Cameron- Martin loss and a Kolmogorov residual is introduced as a PDE diagnostic during inference. These substitutions yield (i) convergence guarantees where the bound's constants depend on the effective rank of the kernel rather than action dimension, (ii) improved trajectory regularity via spectral weighting, and (iii) a deterministic failure detector without reward signals. Validation across two application domains demonstrates substantial improvements: on the PushT manipulation benchmark, the Cameron-Martin loss achieves a 17% improvement in maximum episode reward (0.95 vs. 0.78 for MSE) and 67.6% reduction in inter-step drifts during inference via the introduced residual magnitude. Similarly, on a 6-station manufacturing line with constant work-in-process (CONWIP) flow control, we achieve 28.4% lower RMSE than classical LSTM baselines; a high starvation-event recall (1.0 in test cycles), and effective bottleneck identification (Precision@1 = 1.0 in test set, 13x signal-to-noise ratio). We then certify the dispatch policies with Hamilton-Jacobi reachability theory which reduces deadlock events by 96% compared to uncontrolled dispatch over 100 simulated runs (351 events prevented).

</details>


### [124] [Looped World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18208)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L. Victor Wei,Qun Zhang,Jinrui Zeng,Bowen Cao,Lingwei Meng,Mocheng Li,Zezhong Wang,Haonan Yin,Naifu Xue,Minyu Chen,Cenyuan Zhang,Zefan Zhang,Hao Wei,Jiawei Zhou,Haoran Xu,Hao Yang,Ronglai Zuo,Tongda Xu,Yonghao Li,Jian Chen,Hebin Wang,Zeyu Gao,Yang Li,Wei Zhao,Qimin Zhong,Siqi Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Leyan Cui,Zhangyu Wang,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopWM 通过参数共享的循环变压器架构，在保持计算效率的同时实现了长达100倍的参数效率提升，并建立了迭代潜在深度作为世界模型的新扩展维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长期模拟中面临根本性矛盾：忠实的长程模拟需要深层计算，但深层模型部署成本高且易产生累积误差。

Method: 提出 Looped World Models (LoopWM)，首个用于世界建模的循环架构，通过参数共享的 transformer 块迭代细化潜在环境状态，实现自适应计算。

Result: 相比传统方法实现高达100倍的参数效率，计算深度可根据每个预测步骤的复杂度自动调整。

Conclusion: LoopWM 建立了迭代潜在深度作为世界模拟的新扩展轴，与模型大小和训练数据规模正交，可能显著推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Current world models face a fundamental tension: faithful long-horizon simulation demands deep computation, but deeper models are expensive to deploy and prone to compounding errors. We resolve this by introducing Looped World Models (LoopWM), which are the first looped architectures for world modelling. Our method iteratively refines latent environment states through a parameter-shared transformer block. This yield up to 100x parameter efficiency over conventional approaches with adaptive computation that automatically scales depth to match the complexity of each prediction step. Orthogonal to scaling model size and training data, LoopWM establishes iterative latent depth as a new scaling axis for world simulation, which might significantly push the community forward.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Dataset Distillation for Classification: Do Distilled Sets Outperform Coresets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18209)
*Trisha Mittal,Akshay Mehra,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模标准化实验，系统评估了数据集蒸馏（DD）方法的实际效果，发现在大规模数据集上，最先进的DD方法在准确率上并不优于甚至弱于核心集选择（CS）方法，且构建成本更高，核心集在数据分布覆盖上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法评估协议不一致（从标准ERM到单/多教师监督），难以区分蒸馏数据的实际效果与评估方式的影响；此外，许多方法声称DD优于核心集选择，基于"将压缩数据集限制为真实样本子集会限制其表达能力"的假设，但缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100、ImageNette三个数据集上，使用三种广泛采用的训练协议，对七种最先进的DD方法与三种CS策略进行标准化对比实验；除准确率外，还从代表性、多样性和质量三个维度评估压缩数据集的特性。

Result: 部分DD方法甚至不如简单随机子集；最先进的DD方法在大规模数据集上仅与核心集相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高；核心集在原始数据分布覆盖上始终优于DD方法。

Conclusion: 当前DD方法的实际优势有限，核心集作为一种计算效率更高的替代方案，在数据中心学习中仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) has emerged as a prominent approach in data centric machine learning, aiming to synthesize compact training sets for efficient training by compressing the information in large datasets into a small number of synthetic samples. However, DD methods are often evaluated under inconsistent evaluation protocols, ranging from standard ERM to single/multi-teacher supervision, making it difficult to isolate the effectiveness of distilled data from evaluation. Moreover, many prior methods claim that DD outperforms data pruning approaches such as coreset selection (CS), based on the assumption that restricting condensed datasets to subsets of real samples fundamentally limits their expressiveness. In this work, we critically evaluate DD methods through large-scale experiments using standardized datasets and evaluation protocols to assess their intrinsic effectiveness. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art (SOTA) DD methods on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette, using three widely adopted training protocols against three CS strategies. Our results show that while some DD methods fail to outperform even simple random subsets, the SOTA DD approaches are comparable to or worse than coresets on large-scale datasets and incur a substantially higher cost for construction. Beyond accuracy, we also evaluate the representativeness, diversity, and quality of condensed sets, and find that coresets consistently achieve better coverage of the original data distribution. These findings highlight the limited practical advantages of current DD methods and show that coresets remain competitive and are often a more computationally efficient alternative for data-centric learning.

</details>


### [126] [Sign-Rank, Index, and List Replicability: Connections and Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18236)
*Ari Blondal,Hamed Hatami,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对概念类的符号秩下界问题进行了理论分析，排除了 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 和列表可复制性数这两个度量之间的关系，证明了 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 受列表可复制性数的线性函数上界约束，从而解决了 Frick、Hosseini 和 Vasileuski 提出的关于符号秩与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 之间强分离的问题。此外，还建立了列表可复制性数与高度、最小星数等组合度量之间的上界关系，并证明了乘积概念类的列表可复制性数满足可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号秩是学习理论中衡量二元概念类能否用低维点和半空间表示的关键指标，但其下界证明极为困难。近期的两种方法分别通过 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 和列表可复制性数来建立下界，然而这两种度量之间的关系尚不明确，特别是 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 与符号秩之间的分离问题尚未解决。

Method: 通过理论推导，首先建立 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 与列表可复制性数之间的序关系（前者受后者线性函数上界约束），进而证明符号秩与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 的强分离。随后，通过组合度量（高度和最小星数）对列表可复制性数进行上界刻画，并证明乘积概念类的列表可复制性数满足可加性。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 是列表可复制性数的线性函数上界，从而解决了符号秩与 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 的强分离问题；建立了列表可复制性数与高度、最小星数之间的上界关系；证明了乘积概念类的列表可复制性数不超过二者之和。

Conclusion: 列表可复制性数是比 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index 更强的下界度量，且可通过组合度量进行有效刻画，其可加性性质为复杂概念类的分析提供了工具。

Abstract: In learning theory, the sign rank of a binary concept class captures the smallest dimension in which it can be represented by points and halfspaces. Despite tremendous interest, lower bounds on sign rank are notoriously difficult to come by. Two recent approaches to the problem establish lower bounds on sign rank by measures that are easier to analyze: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and the list replicability number.
  We order these measures, showing that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is upper-bounded by a linear function of the list replicability number. As a main consequence, we obtain a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, thereby resolving a question of Frick, Hosseini, and Vasileuski.
  This motivates a thorough study of list replicability, the stronger of the two lower-bounding measures. We establish upper bounds on the list replicability number by two combinatorial measures: height and minimum star number. We also prove a fundamental composition result, showing that the product of two concept classes has list replicability number bounded by the sum of the list replicability numbers of the two classes.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Collaborative Large and Small Language Models for Accurate and Scalable Data Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17582)
*Qian Chen,Jianwei Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出LasRepair及其扩展版本（LasRepair+, LasRepair++），一种通过大语言模型（LLM）指导小语言模型（SLM）进行数据修复的协作框架。LLM作为指导员选择全局修复上下文，SLM作为修正器进行高效修复；进一步采用EM过程和列校准置信度加权策略提升修复质量和可靠性。理论分析和实验表明，LasRepair++在F1分数上平均提升18.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的数据修复方法存在两个局限：(i) 直接在原始低质量上下文中修复数据，可能损害学习信号；(ii) 直接使用不确定的模型输出作为修复结果，引入不可靠的修正，降低修复质量。为此，需要一种更高效、更可靠的修复方法。

Method: LasRepair框架：LLM作为指导员，选择全局修复上下文；SLM作为修正器，利用选定的上下文高效修复错误数据。LasRepair+：将数据修复建模为EM过程，E步更新修正器参数，M步精炼修复上下文。LasRepair++：引入列校准的模型置信度，对不可靠的修复行进行降权处理，在更新修正器时减轻模型不确定性的影响。

Result: 理论分析证明了EM过程和置信度加权的有效性。在真实数据集上的实验表明，LasRepair++相比最强基线实现了平均18.1%的F1分数提升。

Conclusion: 通过大模型指导小模型的协作范式，结合EM迭代优化和置信度加权策略，能够有效提升数据修复的质量和可靠性，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of data repair, a key task in data cleaning that corrects erroneous entries in raw datasets to improve overall data quality. Although recent data-driven methods, especially those based on large language models (LLMs), achieve remarkable performance, we observe that: (i) they directly repair data in the raw and low-quality context, which may compromise learning signals, and (ii) they directly use uncertain model outputs as repairs, potentially introducing unreliable corrections and compromising repair quality. Motivated by the efficiency of small language models (SLMs) and the capabilities of LLMs, and aiming to address the above limitations, we propose LasRepair, a framework that collaborates Large and small language models for data repair. LasRepair employs an LLM as an instructor, which selects a global repair context to guide the SLM. The SLM acts as a corrector, using the selected context to repair erroneous data more efficiently. Moreover, to further improve context quality, we extend LasRepair to LasRepair+, which formulates data repair as an Expectation-Maximisation (EM) procedure that alternates between an E-step for updating the corrector parameters and an M-step for refining the repair context. Furthermore, to mitigate model uncertainty, we propose LasRepair++, which uses column-calibrated model confidence to down-weight unreliable repaired rows when updating the corrector, thereby enhancing repair quality. Theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation demonstrate the superiority of our methods. We theoretically prove the effectiveness of the EM-style procedure and the confidence-based weighting. Experiments on real-world datasets show that LasRepair++~ achieves an average F1-score improvement of 18.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [128] [The Value of Adaptivity in LSM Bloom-Filter Tuning: A Log-Law and a Two-Clock Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18138)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了LSM树中自适应布隆过滤器内存分配的价值，发现仅在压缩时重新分配即可捕获96-99%的追踪收益，并提出了三区域策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSM树的过滤器的自适应内存分配虽被广泛研究，但其相对于静态分配的真正收益何时值得其额外复杂性尚未明确，作者想回答自适应分配何时值得其开销这一前置问题。

Method: 通过三个分析结果：对数律（最优bits-per-key与访问频率对数呈仿射关系，斜率固定）、鲁棒律（热估计误差导致的额外读取成本为对数误差的加权方差的一半，粗略估计损失很小）、自适应价值边界（连续追踪的价值与epoch内漂移的二次方成正比，存在闭式标度），并在合成数据、Twitter生产缓存追踪和真实RocksDB引擎上验证。

Result: 在真实集群上，仅在压缩时重新分配能捕获96-99%的连续追踪收益；在RocksDB上，假阳性率在每键4-8比特内误差不超过4%。

Conclusion: 提出了一个三区域策略（粗粒度压缩时分配即可、需要追踪、极端漂移时回退到均匀分配），并预测更多倾斜反而使细粒度追踪的重要性降低。贡献在于刻画了自适应调优何时有价值，未添加新过滤器也未修改引擎。

Abstract: Log-structured merge (LSM) trees attach an approximate-membership filter to every run and must split a fixed memory budget across them. The static optimum is known (Monkey); a large systems literature then makes the allocation adaptive, tracking shifting hotness online. We ask a prior question: when is that adaptivity worth its machinery? We give three analytical answers and validate them on synthetic sweeps, real Twitter production cache traces, and a real RocksDB engine. First, a log-law: optimal bits-per-key is affine in the logarithm of access frequency, at a fixed slope. Second, a robustness law: because the workload enters only logarithmically, the excess read cost from a hotness misestimate is half the size-weighted variance of the log error, and a common-factor misestimate is absorbed by the budget multiplier, so coarse estimates lose little. Third, an adaptivity-value frontier: since compaction rebuilds filters for free on its own clock, the value of continuous tracking over an allocation recomputed only at compaction grows quadratically in the within-epoch drift, with a closed-form scale. This yields a three-regime policy (coarse-at-compaction suffices, then track, then at extreme drift fall back to uniform) and predicts that more skew makes fine tracking matter less. On a real cluster, reallocating only at compaction captures 96-99% of tracking's benefit; on RocksDB the false-positive primitive holds within four percent to eight bits per key. The contribution is a characterization of when adaptive tuning pays; we add no new filter and no engine fork. Code and pre-registration are public.

</details>


### [129] [Group Commit Self-Clocks: Why Tuning Is Unnecessary Above a Device-Set Load Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18187)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文通过闭排队网络模型分析，证明在封闭环OLTP环境下，参数无关的贪婪流水线策略（立即刷新）性能接近最优调优定时器（误差约0.1%），而经典的平方根规则和滑雪租赁策略的有效性被限制在特定负载阈值以下，解释了为什么生产实践中commit_delay=0是合理选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑经典分组提交调优理论（EOQ平方根规则、滑雪租赁2-竞争比）在真实闭环OLTP系统中的适用性，揭示开放环路假设与实际负载模式之间的根本性错配。

Method: 将事务提交建模为闭排队网络（客户端在提交后才发起下一事务），推导出负载阈值λ*=2/F₀，在此阈值以上定时器策略退化为贪婪策略；在AWS EBS gp3和实例NVMe两种存储上测量fsync延迟分布，并在PostgreSQL上验证commit_delay=0的实际表现。

Result: 贪婪流水线策略在所有负载下与最优oracle调优定时器的性能差距仅约0.1%；平方根规则仅在λ<λ*且开放环假设下才有效；在闭环系统中，参数无关的ski策略仍优于固定调优定时器。PostgreSQL实验确认commit_delay=0具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 经典调优理论在闭环OLTP中基本失效，参数无关策略是更优的实际选择；贡献在于理论刻画解释了现有工程实践，而非提出新的日志记录器。

Abstract: Group commit amortizes the fixed cost of a durable log flush across many committing transactions; the release rule - a timer, a batch size, or an adaptive policy - is a classic tuning knob. The textbook theory is open-loop: for Poisson arrivals the optimal timer is the EOQ square-root rule, and the wait-or-flush decision is ski-rental 2-competitive. We ask when that tuning is worth its machinery, and show that in closed-loop OLTP it usually is not. Real commit arrivals are closed-loop: a client issues its next transaction only after its last commits, so the arrival rate is induced by the policy's own latency. Modeling this as a closed queueing network, the parameter-free greedy-pipelined policy (flush the instant the device is free) self-clocks to a computable fixed point and is within about 0.1% of the best oracle-tuned timer at every load. The square-root rule prescribes waiting $T^\star=\sqrt{2F_0/λ}$, but $T^\star<F_0$ exactly when $λ>λ^\star=2/F_0$; above this device-set load threshold the timer collapses onto greedy and tuning is vacuous. The clean theory only bites below $λ^\star$ and in the open-loop world, where a parameter-free ski policy still beats a fixed tuned timer under rate shifts. We instantiate $λ^\star$ with measured fsync distributions on two AWS storage classes (EBS gp3 versus instance NVMe, a $25\times$ range), and confirm on PostgreSQL that commit_delay=0 is competitive with any tuned value. The contribution is a characterization that explains deployed practice; we add no new logger.

</details>


### [130] [A benchmark suite of intracellular Boolean model variants and multiscale simulations for computational biology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18215)
*Marco Masera,Riccardo Smeriglio,Roberta Bardini,Alessandro Savino,Stefano Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PhysiBench是一个开源资源，包含612个可执行的布尔调控网络变体和12万条多尺度随机模拟轨迹，用于系统生物学方法的开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前系统生物学领域缺乏标准化的开源基准资源，用于跨方法（如直接模拟、替代建模、数据驱动推理）的公平比较和验证。

Method: 从7个已发表的布尔网络（涵盖细胞周期、发育模式、癌症信号等）出发，通过突变生成、在线行为过滤和离线敏感性评估构建612个模型变体；在PhysiBoSS/PhysiCell框架下，对60个选定模型在不同刺激协议下生成12万条多尺度随机模拟轨迹。

Result: 生成了包含612个模型变体的基准套件和12万条高分辨率时间轨迹数据集，每条轨迹均关联模型ID、输入参数、随机种子和细胞级输出文件，并通过完整性检查、结构多样性分析和行为异质性评估完成了技术验证。

Conclusion: PhysiBench为直接模拟、替代建模、数据驱动推理、仿真优化和比较基准测试提供了标准化平台，填补了系统生物学多尺度方法评估的资源空白。

Abstract: We present PhysiBench, an open resource for developing and evaluating computational methods in systems biology including a benchmark suite of 612 executable intracellular Boolean regulatory network variants and a dataset of 120,000 time-resolved multiscale stochastic simulations. The benchmark models are derived from seven published Boolean networks spanning cell-cycle control, developmental patterning, cancer signaling, immune response, and cell-fate decisions, and are executable in the PhysiBoSS/PhysiCell multiscale simulation framework. Model variants are generated through mutation-based model construction, online behavioral filtering, and offline sensitivity evaluation. The simulation dataset is produced from 60 selected models under systematically sampled stimulation protocols and fixed model-level initial configurations. Each trajectory is linked to its model identifier, input-parameter file, stochastic seed, and cell-level output file. PhysiBench supports direct simulation, surrogate modeling, data-driven inference, simulation-based optimization, and comparative benchmarking. Technical validation includes file-integrity and executability checks, graph-based structural diversity analyses, and behavioral heterogeneity assessment from multiscale simulation outputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [ZIVARI-TLBO: A Zero-Cost Inter-Group Evaluated-Elite Relay Mechanism for Teaching-Learning-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17087)
*Pezhman Zivari*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: ZIVARI-TLBO是一种改进的教与学优化方法，通过在分组TLBO中引入固定环精英中继机制，在不增加评估次数的情况下实现信息共享。在标准函数和工程问题上与多种算法对比，表现中等偏上，略逊于WOA，但显著优于TLBO、DE、PSO等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分组TLBO框架中，各子群之间缺乏高效且零额外评估开销的信息共享机制，现有方法要么引入额外计算成本，要么难以平衡探索与利用。

Method: 在已有的分组景观感知控制器基础上，增加一个固定环的组间精英中继：每个组将其已评估的精英个体传递给环中的下一个组，当精英的目标值优于接收组中最差合格学习者的目标值时，执行替换。由于精英及其适应度值已被评估过，该中继不引入额外目标函数调用。

Result: 在8个经典函数（维度10/30/50/100）和5个工程问题上，与无中继的消融基线对比取得728/11/221胜/平/负，秩双列效应量0.624。八方法多维比较中WOA平均秩2.914最优，ZIVARI-TLBO以3.382位居第二，显著优于TLBO、MCTLBO、DE、PSO、GWO，显著逊于WOA，与HHO无显著差异。工程问题结果混合。

Conclusion: ZIVARI-TLBO的中继机制在预算一致的信息共享方面有效，但并非通用最优解，不支持全局收敛、工程主导或CEC优越性等过度声明。

Abstract: ZIVARI-TLBO is a grouped Teaching-Learning-Based Optimization (TLBO) method that augments an existing population-state controller with a fixed inter-group evaluated-elite relay. At each scheduled event, every group offers its already evaluated elite to the next group in a fixed ring; the elite replaces the receiver's worst eligible learner only when its stored objective value is better. Because the exact relay copies an already evaluated solution and its stored fitness, it requires no additional objective-function calls. The frozen gts-v4-cm-fixed implementation is evaluated under equal 10,000-evaluation budgets on eight classical functions at dimensions 10, 30, 50, and 100, with 30 matched seeds, and on five constrained engineering problems. A direct ablation against the same grouped landscape-aware controller without relay records 728/11/221 wins/ties/losses and a rank-biserial effect size of 0.624 across dimensions. In an eight-method multidimensional comparison, WOA obtains the best average rank (2.914) and ZIVARI-TLBO ranks second (3.382); ZIVARI-TLBO significantly outperforms TLBO, MCTLBO, DE, PSO, and GWO, loses significantly to WOA, and is not significantly different from HHO after Holm adjustment. Feasibility-aware engineering results are mixed and sensitive to the current static-penalty formulation. The evidence supports a scoped relay contribution and budget-consistent information-sharing mechanism, but not universal state-of-the-art, global-convergence, engineering-dominance, or CEC superiority claims.

</details>


### [132] [Directing Open-Ended Evolution in Artificial Life via Multi-Scale Path Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17091)
*Mikhail Akhtyrchenko,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Andrey Ustyuzhanin*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出MSPD（多尺度路径散度），一种受重整化群启发的标量度量，可量化局部转移律在时间多尺度上的异质性组织。MSPD作为显式公式，既能作为无梯度适应度函数引导开放式进化，又能作为事后分析工具揭示局部转移律，在多个人工生命基板（Flow-Lenia、类生命元胞自动机、Particle Life++）上验证了其有效性，为开放式进化中的复杂度度量提供了与物理理论相连的原则性桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式进化（OEE）中现有复杂度度量依赖无法解释的黑盒神经网络指标，使得类生命系统与复杂性的物理理论脱节。作者希望提出一种具有物理基础、可解释且能双向发挥作用（引导进化+事后分析）的显式复杂度度量。

Method: 基于重整化群思想，定义多尺度路径散度（MSPD/DP）为群体层面轨迹泛函，通过窗口有限分辨率估计器计算，并证明两者一致性。该度量是显式公式，可作为无梯度适应度函数引导进化，也可作为事后分析工具刻画局部转移律。

Result: MSPD优化参数在保留集上产生高于随机参数的复杂度分数；高MSPD状态对外部干预更不稳定，表明度量追踪的是底层动力学生物学特性而非噪声；高MSPD对应更强的尺度依赖挫折（不同空间尺度下动力学差异更大），与Vanchurin等人的生物学复杂度挫折准则直接关联；该协议可迁移至Life-like元胞自动机和Particle Life++。

Conclusion: 一个显式公式即可同时引导开放式进化并提供与复杂度物理学之间的原则性桥梁，这是黑盒驱动方法所不能实现的。

Abstract: Open-ended evolution (OEE) in artificial life is typically driven by uninterpretable, black-box neural-network complexity metrics, leaving life-like systems disconnected from physical theories of complexity. We introduce MSPD (Multi-Scale Path Divergence, denoted DP ), a renormalization-group-inspired scalar that quantifies the temporal multiscale organization of heterogeneity in local transition laws. MSPD is defined at the population level as a functional of the realised trajectory and is computed as a windowed finite-resolution estimator, with consistency between the two stated as a proposition. The metric is an explicit formula and plays a dual role: as a gradient-free fitness function and as a post-hoc analytical lens on any simulation that exposes local transition laws. Empirically, MSPD-optimized parameters produce higher held-out complexity scores than matched random parameters from the same substrate. High-$H_{Delta_t}$ states correspond to states with higher instability to external interventions, so the metric tracks the biology of the underlying dynamics rather than noise. Higher MSPD corresponds to stronger scale-dependent frustration: high-complexity systems exhibit larger differences between the dynamics expressed at different spatial extents, linking MSPD directly to the frustration criterion of biological complexity in the sense of Vanchurin et al. [ 23 ]. The same protocol transfers beyond the primary Flow-Lenia substrate to Life-like cellular automata and Particle Life++, where C1, C2 and C5 all hold. A single explicit formula thus both directs open-ended evolution and provides a principled bridge to the physics of complexity that black-box drivers do not.

</details>


### [133] [Evolutionary Algorithms and Multi-Objective Minimum Spanning Trees with Limited Distinct Weight Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17731)
*Narges Tavassoli Kejani,Andrew M. Sutton,Frank Neumann*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标最小生成树问题中Pareto前沿的结构特征，特别是当边权取值较少时的情形，并基于这些结构洞察推导了进化多目标算法的新运行时间结果，同时通过实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标组合优化问题中进化算法在实际中表现成功，但理论运行时间分析非常有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，深入分析多目标最小生成树问题的Pareto前沿结构，以推导更精确的算法运行时间上界。

Method: 通过分析边权取值较少时Pareto前沿的极值角点结构，利用结构特性推导进化多目标算法（如GSEMO）的运行时间上界，并通过实验验证理论结果。

Result: 获得了在边权取值有限时Pareto前沿的更细致结构描述，并基于此推导了进化多目标算法的新运行时间上界，实验与理论结果一致。

Conclusion: 通过对Pareto前沿结构的深入分析，可以在特定条件下改进进化多目标算法求解多目标最小生成树问题的理论运行时间上界，实验验证了理论分析的有效性。

Abstract: Evolutionary algorithms have been used for a wide range of multi-objective combinatorial optimization problems. Despite practical success, theoretical results on the runtime of evolutionary algorithms for multi-objective combinatorial problems are rather limited. One classical problem that has been investigated is the multi-objective minimum spanning tree problem for which runtime bounds have been obtained to compute all extremal corner points of the Pareto front. With this paper, we provide some more detailed insights into the structure of the Pareto front when the edge weights take on a small number of distinct values. Based on these insights, we derive new runtime results for evolutionary multi-objective algorithms and complement our theoretical results with experimental investigations.

</details>


### [134] [An Optimization Framework for Automated Assessment of Biological Plausibility of Spiking Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17853)
*Sven Nitzsche,Alexandru Ionita,Andreas Faust,Bogdan Ionescu,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源框架，用于自动评估脉冲神经元模型的生物合理性，通过将标准神经元放电模式编码为目标函数并优化参数，实现黑盒评估，兼容PyTorch和Norse库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物合理性在神经形态计算和脉冲神经网络中是一个关键概念，但其定义不一致且难以量化，缺乏系统化的评估方法。

Method: 基于Izhikevich提出的标准神经元放电模式分类，将这些模式编码为目标函数，通过优化模型参数自动评估生物合理性，将神经元模型视为黑盒进行处理。

Result: 在多个已建立模型和一个先前未探索的自定义模型上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统研究生物合理性与网络级性能指标（如精度、能效、鲁棒性和适应性）之间的关系提供了起点。

Abstract: Biological plausibility is a key concept in neuromorphic computing and spiking neural networks, yet it remains inconsistently defined and difficult to quantify. In this work, we present an open-source framework for the automated assessment of biological plausibility in spiking neuron models. Our method builds on the idea of evaluating a model's ability to replicate canonical neuronal firing patterns observed in biological systems, following the classification proposed by Izhikevich. By encoding these patterns into objective functions and optimizing model parameters accordingly, our framework enables empirical assessment without requiring prior analytical modeling. Treating neuron models as black boxes, it provides a practical and flexible means of characterizing their dynamic capabilities. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework on several established models and a previously unexplored custom model. Implemented in Python and compatible with PyTorch and the Norse library, the framework is tailored for machine learning contexts. It is intended as a starting point for systematic research into the relationship between biological plausibility and network-level performance metrics such as accuracy, energy efficiency, robustness, and adaptability.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Agent Utilities over Generalized Voronoi Regions and their Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17388)
*Andre N. Costa,Petter Ögren,Carlos H. C. Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了代价诱导的Voronoi（CIV）区域概念，将代理效用定义为CIV区域上效用密度的积分，利用雷诺输运定理计算效用梯度，并在足球双团队场景中验证了该方法相比有限差分近似在保持精度的同时将计算时间降低约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Voronoi区域假设代理状态空间与被划分空间相同，无法处理状态空间（如位置+速度）与划分空间（仅位置）不同的场景。作者希望推广Voronoi区域概念，使其适用于更一般的代价函数（如LQR最优控制解），并在此基础上定义代理效用及其梯度，以实现高效优化。

Method: 1) 提出Cost-Induced Voronoi (CIV)区域，允许代理状态空间与划分空间不同，代价函数可以是任意形式（如LQR最优控制解）；2) 将代理效用定义为CIV区域上效用密度（如传球接球概率密度）的积分；3) 利用流体力学中的雷诺输运定理推导效用梯度的解析表达式，替代传统的有限差分近似。

Result: 所提出的基于雷诺输运定理的梯度计算方法与有限差分基线方法相比，在达到相似计算精度的同时，计算时间降低了约一个数量级。

Conclusion: CIV区域框架有效扩展了经典Voronoi区域的应用范围，特别适用于代理状态空间与关注空间不一致的场景。雷诺输运定理为计算这类区域上的效用梯度提供了一种高效且精确的解析方法，在机器人学、多智能体系统等领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we generalize the concept of Voronoi regions, define agent utility as the integral of a utility density over the corresponding Voronoi region, derive gradients of the utility, and illustrate the approach in a two-team example from soccer.
  The generalization of Voronoi regions is in the form of so-called Cost-Induced Voronoi (CIV) regions, where the agent state space may differ from the space being partitioned. One example of such regions is when the cost is given by the optimal solution of an LQR control problem. Then the agent states include position as well as velocity, while the partitioned space only includes positions.
  The agent utility is defined by integrating some utility density over the CIV region of the agent. This utility density might be the probability density of some beneficial event, such as receiving a pass in soccer. The utility is then the overall probability of receiving a pass and the gradient represents a way to improve that probability. We show how this utility gradient can be computed using the Reynolds Transport Theorem from fluid mechanics, and that this approach achieves similar accuracy while reducing computation time by about an order of magnitude compared to a baseline finite-difference approximation.

</details>


### [136] [Extracting Semantics: LLM-Guided Automatic Population of Robot Ontology from URDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17073)
*Bastien Dussard,Guillaume Sarthou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型(LLM)从URDF文件自动生成机器人语义本体知识库的方法，通过多数投票和语法模式验证确保可靠性，初步实验表明该方法能有效桥接低层机器人描述与高层语义知识表示之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要具备具身化和语义丰富的环境与自身表征以支持人机交互，但本体的手工构建效率低下，成为发展瓶颈。

Method: 提出一个自动化流水线：将URDF模型转换为本体知识库，利用LLM通过提示工程技术推断语义关系，结合多数投票与语法/模式验证确保输出符合本体约束。

Result: 在多个机器人描述上进行了评估，初步结果表明该方法能有效弥合低层描述与结构化知识表示之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动生成符合形式化本体模型的机器人语义抽象，为可解释的人机交互提供支撑。

Abstract: While commonsense knowledge may suffice for virtual agents, embodied robots interacting with humans require grounded and semantically rich representations of both their environment and their own physical embodiment. In cognitive robotics, ontologies are effective for integrating such heterogeneous knowledge to enable explainable reasoning, even during continuous knowledge updates. Yet, their manual construction remains a bottleneck. We present a preliminary approach for the automatic generation of robot semantic abstractions by transforming Unified Robot Description Format (URDF) models into populated ontologies. Although URDF files provide structural and kinematic descriptions, their identifiers often require commonsense interpretation to recover meaningful semantics, a task at which Large Language Models (LLMs) excel. Our pipeline leverages LLMs to infer semantic relationships by prompting them with concepts from an existing ontology, ensuring the final classification remains aligned with the formal model. To improve reliability, the pipeline combines majority voting across multiple LLM queries along with syntactic and schema-level validation to ensure that generated outputs conform to the expected representation format and ontology constraints. We evaluate the approach on multiple robot descriptions and discuss the generated abstractions. Initial results indicate that the proposed method can effectively bridge the gap between low-level robot descriptions and the structured, grounded knowledge representations required for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [137] [HRDX: A Large-Scale Vector HD-Map Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17080)
*Sahith Reddy Chada,Isht Dwivedi,Nirav Savaliya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRDX是一个大规模高清矢量地图数据集，覆盖约40小时/1400公里的驾驶数据，包含6个环绕摄像头、128线激光雷达、厘米级RTK GNSS/IMU以及精确对齐的航空正射影像。标注涵盖10种矢量地图类别和20+语义/拓扑属性，并提出Composite Score(CS)联合评估几何精度与属性正确性。实验表明其规模优势及航空影像作为结构先验的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开高清地图数据集规模有限、语义属性稀疏、缺乏航空影像等多模态数据，限制了自动驾驶中矢量化HD地图构建的研究方向。

Method: 构建了大规模数据集HRDX（40小时/1400公里），配备6路环绕相机、128线激光雷达、厘米级RTK GNSS/IMU及对齐航空正射影像；标注10种矢量类别和20+语义/拓扑属性；提出Composite Score(CS)联合评估几何精度与属性正确性。

Result: HRDX的规模提升了在线矢量地图构建性能；航空影像在训练和/或推理时使用可改善几何地图质量；航空增强教师模型可将部分优势迁移至仅使用相机的学生模型，且不增加推理时传感器需求。

Conclusion: HRDX旨在支持大规模HD地图学习、多模态BEV融合以及训练时特权信息的可重复研究。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous driving requires vectorized HD maps that are geometrically accurate, semantically rich, and scalable to long-horizon driving. However, existing public HD map datasets are limited in scale, provide sparse semantic attributes, and lack modalities such as aerial imagery that could enable new research directions. We present HRDX, a large-scale dataset for vector HD-map construction, spanning about 40 hours (1,400 km) of minimally overlapping drives, which is several times larger than prior public HD map datasets. Data is captured using six synchronized surround cameras, a 128-beam LiDAR, and centimeter-level RTK GNSS/IMU, and is further complemented by precisely aligned aerial orthoimagery. Annotations cover 10 vector map classes, complemented with over 20 semantic and topological attributes. To evaluate this richer ontology, we introduce the Composite Score (CS) to jointly assess geometric fidelity and attribute correctness. Benchmark experiments show that HRDX's scale improves online vector-map construction, and that aligned aerial imagery provides a useful structural prior: using aerial imagery at training and/or inference improves geometric map quality, while aerial-augmented teachers can transfer part of this benefit to camera-only students without increasing inference-time sensor requirements. HRDX is intended to support reproducible research on large-scale HD-map learning, multimodal BEV fusion, and training-time privileged information. HRDX dataset and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/honda-research-institute/HRDX

</details>


### [138] [ParkingTransformer: LLM-Enhanced End-to-End Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17082)
*Hauteng Wu,Xu Li,Dong Kong,Zihang Wang,Xieyuanli Chen,Benwu Wang,Wenkai Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ParkingTransformer框架，利用多视角感知和大语言模型实现端到端自主泊车，通过轨迹查询与LLM隐状态特征交互、3D位置编码、固定窗口流式机制和由粗到细解码策略，在CARLA仿真和实车平台上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自主泊车方法存在黑盒特性，缺乏高层语义理解和可解释性，阻碍了从道路到目标点的无缝长距离自主泊车的实现。

Method: 1) 利用轨迹查询与LLM隐状态特征直接与历史信息和原始传感器数据交互，无需密集BEV表示；2) 引入3D位置编码显式注入空间几何感知；3) 设计固定窗口流式机制处理历史信息；4) 采用由粗到细解码策略逐步提升轨迹精度。

Result: CARLA仿真中驾驶得分为61.32，实车实验中平均成功率达到88.70%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在仿真和真实场景中均验证了可行性和有效性，实现了高可解释性的长距离自主泊车。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous parking has emerged as a critical task within the realm of autonomous driving. However, existing methods suffer from black-box characteristics, lacking high-level semantic understanding and interpretability, which impedes the realization of seamless long-distance autonomous parking from the road to the target spot. To address these limitations, we propose ParkingTransformer, a novel framework that leverages multi-view perception and the scene understanding capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). By combining trajectory queries with LLMs implicit state features, our method interacts directly with historical information and raw sensor data to output planning trajectories, eliminating the need for dense Bird's-View (BEV) representations. To compensate for the inadequate spatial reasoning ability of LLMs, we introduce 3D positional encoding to explicitly inject spatial geometric awareness. Furthermore, a fixed-window streaming mechanism is designed for historical information processing, significantly improving long-term temporal processing efficiency and inference speed. Additionally, a coarse-to-fine decoding strategy is employed to progressively enhance trajectory precision. Extensive closed-loop experiments are conducted on the CARLA simulator and real-world vehicle platforms. The results demonstrate that our method achieves a driving score of 61.32 in CARLA simulator and an average success rate of 88.70% in real-world experiments, validating the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [139] [VL-MemKnG: Hybrid Memory with a Spatio-Temporal Knowledge Graph for Question Answering over Long Egocentric Navigation Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17183)
*Svetlana Lukina,Mohamad Al Mdfaa,Gloria Haro,Sergey Zagoruyko,Gonzalo Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VL-MemKnG混合记忆框架，将时空知识图谱与片段级上下文记忆结合，在长视频导航问答任务中提升检索准确率（Top-1从58%到67%），优于Gemini 2.5 Pro等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文视觉语言模型虽能取得高回答质量，但在处理长轨迹时计算成本高且重复查询效率低；现有图方法（如VL-KnG）依赖图检索，可能忽视更广泛的时间连续性和上下文线索。

Method: 提出VL-MemKnG混合记忆框架，包含：(1) 时空知识图谱捕获结构化关系信息与长程物体关联；(2) 持久片段级上下文记忆保留更广泛的时间上下文；(3) 混合检索与推理模块联合操作两种记忆表示以生成有证据支撑的答案。同时构建WalkingKnowledgeT+基准扩展长视界导航问答任务。

Result: 在WalkieKnowledgeT+上，VL-MemKnG将Top-1检索准确率从58%提升至67%，Recall@1从34.50%提升至40.55%，超越所有对比方法（包括Gemini 2.5 Pro和Qwen 3.5+），在时间全局和时间分散聚合类问题上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 结合结构化关系记忆与片段级上下文记忆能有效提升长视频导航问答的检索与推理性能，同时保持高效的查询推理效率。

Abstract: Answering navigation-relevant questions over long egocentric videos requires retrieving and organizing evidence distributed across distant temporal moments while maintaining spatial and contextual consistency. Although long-context vision--language models can achieve strong answer quality, they are computationally expensive for long trajectories and inefficient for repeated querying. Recent graph-based approaches such as VL-KnG address this challenge through persistent spatio-temporal knowledge graphs, but graph-centric retrieval alone may underrepresent broader temporal continuity and contextual cues. We present VL-MemKnG, a hybrid memory framework that extends VL-KnG by combining a spatio-temporal knowledge graph with persistent segment-level contextual memory. The knowledge graph captures structured relational information and long-range object associations, while segment-level memory preserves broader temporal context for long-horizon evidence retrieval. A hybrid retrieval-and-reasoning module jointly operates over both memory representations to produce evidence-grounded answers and temporally organized supporting evidence. We also introduce WalkieKnowledgeT+, an extension of WalkieKnowledge for long-horizon navigation-oriented video question answering. The benchmark includes temporally distributed reasoning tasks requiring evidence aggregation across multiple non-cooccurring moments. On WalkieKnowledgeT+, VL-MemKnG improves Top-1 retrieval accuracy from 58% to 67% and Recall@1 from 34.50% to 40.55%, outperforming all compared methods, including Gemini 2.5 Pro and Qwen 3.5+. The gains are particularly pronounced on temporal-global and temporally scattered aggregation questions, demonstrating the benefits of combining structured relational memory with segment-level contextual memory while maintaining efficient query-time inference.

</details>


### [140] [ACE-Ego-0: Unifying Egocentric Human and Robotic Data for VLA Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17200)
*Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Yufei Liu,Haotian Hou,Guoquan Ye,Tongyan Fang,Chunxiao Liu,Siyuan Huang,Jianbo Liu,Xiaogang Wang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-EGO-0是一个统一的VLA预训练框架，通过将大规模第一人称人类视频转化为伪动作轨迹，结合可靠性感知训练目标，有效联合异构数据（4.53K小时机器人+模拟数据与1.48K小时人类视频）进行预训练，在多个基准和真实世界双臂操作任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹数据采集成本高昂且劳动密集，而大规模第一人称人类视频蕴含着丰富的真实世界监督信号，但人类数据与机器人数据在动作空间、具身结构、时间动态和监督质量上存在显著差异，直接联合训练面临挑战。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展的第一人称视频到动作管线，将原始人类视频转化为机器人格式的伪动作轨迹；2) 提出统一动作表示，基于相机空间动作、形态条件化和时间对齐的动作分块；3) 设计可靠性感知训练目标及人类辅助损失，集中监督于可靠信号。

Result: 在RoboCasa GR1 TableTop和RoboTwin 2.0上达到SOTA性能，并展示了向真实世界双臂操作任务的强迁移能力。

Conclusion: 大规模人类监督结合可靠性感知加权方法能够有效提升统一的联合预训练和有监督微调性能，验证了利用异构数据源进行VLA预训练的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models benefit from large-scale and diverse embodied data, yet scaling robot trajectory collection is costly and labor-intensive. Recent advances show that large-scale egocentric human videos provide complementary real-world supervision in pretraining. However, joint training on human and robot data remains challenging due to divergences in action spaces, embodiment structures, temporal dynamics, and supervision quality. We introduce ACE-EGO-0, a unified VLA pretraining framework jointly leveraging heterogeneous data sources. To extract large-scale pretraining supervision from egocentric human videos, we build a scalable egocentric video-to-action pipeline that converts raw human videos into robot-format pseudo-action trajectories. To make these labels comparable with robot demonstrations, ACE-EGO-0 uses a unified action representation based on camera-space actions, morphology conditioning, and time-aligned action chunking. To robustly leverage noisy pseudo-action supervision from egocentric human videos, we formulate a reliability-aware training objective with a human auxiliary loss that concentrates supervision on reliable signals. We instantiate ACE-EGO-0 on 4.53K hours of robot and simulation data, together with 1.48K hours of pseudo-action-labeled egocentric human data. Experiments show that incorporating large-scale human supervision under reliability-aware weighting consistently improves both unified joint pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. ACE-EGO-0 achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa GR1 TableTop and RoboTwin 2.0, while demonstrating strong transfer to real-world bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [141] [Contrastive Action-Image Pre-training for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17256)
*Yuvan Sharma,Dantong Niu,Anirudh Pai,Zekai Wang,Zhuoyang Liu,Baifeng Shi,Stefano Saravalle,Boning Shao,Ruijie Zheng,Jing Wang,Konstantinos Kallidromitis,Yusuke Kato,Fabio Galasso,Yuke Zhu,Danfei Xu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Jitendra Malik,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAIP是一种对比式动作-图像预训练方法，通过从大规模自我中心视频中提取3D手部关键点作为末端执行器动作的代理，学习统一的动作-图像表征，在仅使用88小时机器人操作数据的情况下，利用32,041小时的人类视频预训练，在灵巧操作任务上取得超过30%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视觉编码器受限于机器人数据集规模不足，而互联网图像/语言数据或自我中心人类视频缺乏与动作的配对信号，无法直接服务于下游视觉运动控制策略。

Method: 从大规模自我中心视频中提取3D手部关键点作为机器人末端执行器动作的代理，通过对比学习目标训练视觉编码器，学习统一的动作-图像表征。

Result: 在Dexmate Vega和Sharpa Wave灵巧手上，CAIP在折叠、倒液体和精细操作任务上相比DINOv2、SigLIP、MVP和R3M等SOTA编码器取得超过30%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 对比式动作中心预训练为实现更适合物理交互的鲁棒视觉表征提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Existing vision encoders for robotics face a fundamental bottleneck: robotic datasets lack the scale necessary for large-scale pre-training. Prior work circumvents this data scarcity by turning to internet-scale image and language data or egocentric human video. While these models show promise, neither paradigm learns from paired vision and action data, which downstream visuomotor control policies require. However, robot trajectories, the most direct source of this paired signal, are not available at pre-training scale, motivating us to extract action signals from abundant human video instead. To this end, we introduce CAIP (Contrastive Action-Image Pre-training), a vision encoder that treats human hand poses from large-scale egocentric video as a proxy for end-effector actions. By extracting 3D hand keypoints, a representation that aligns naturally with downstream robot action spaces, CAIP learns a unified action-image representation through a contrastive objective. Leveraging 32,041 hours of egocentric human video and only 88 hours of robotic manipulation data, CAIP outperforms state-of-the-art vision encoders including DINOv2, SigLIP, MVP, and R3M. Evaluated on a challenging real-world dexterous manipulation setup using Dexmate Vega and Sharpa Wave hands, CAIP yields performance gains of more than 30% on tasks involving folding, pouring, and fine-grained manipulation. Our results show that our method of contrastive action-centric pre-training yields a scalable path to achieving robust visual representations better suited for physical interaction.

</details>


### [142] [VISTA: Scale-Aware Visual Navigation via Action History Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17294)
*Maeva Guerrier,Koki Kobayashi,Simon Roy,Jana Pavlasek,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA，一种视觉导航基础模型，通过条件化归一化动作历史解决缩放因子导致的部署脆弱性问题，并集成DINOv3编码器提升在视觉重复环境中的表现。在零样本真实世界部署中达到100%目标预测准确率和95%平均检查点通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航基础模型预测归一化动作时，不同缩放因子会改变轨迹的物理几何结构，导致导航性能下降和碰撞风险增加；同时，现有模型在视觉重复环境中表现不佳。

Method: 1) 将模型条件化于归一化动作历史与图像观测，显式提供模型预测与机器人实际物理位移之间的关系上下文；2) 集成DINOv3编码器，利用其更丰富的表征能力捕获观测之间的空间和几何维度。

Result: 在零样本真实世界部署中，Outdoor、Forest和Office场景下达到100%目标预测准确率，平均95%检查点被成功穿越，证明了在未见环境中一致的路径跟踪能力。

Conclusion: VISTA通过动作历史条件化和DINOv3编码器，有效解决了归一化动作的部署脆弱性和视觉重复环境下的导航难题，实现了跨场景的鲁棒零样本导航。

Abstract: Vision Navigation Foundation Models (VNMs) promise end-to-end learned navigation policies capable of zero-shot deployment across diverse embodiments and environments. To maintain generality, many vision-based navigation models predict normalized actions. However, this normalization introduces a critical deployment vulnerability: applying different scaling factors to the same normalized trajectory alters its physical geometry, which degrades navigation performance and increases collision risks. We address this vulnerability by conditioning the model on normalized action histories alongside image observations, providing explicit context on the relationship between the model's predictions and the robot's actual physical displacement. Furthermore, current VNMs often struggle in visually repetitive environments that lack distinct features. To resolve this issue, we integrate a DINOv3 encoder, whose richer representations enable our model to capture both spatial and geometric dimensions between observations. VISTA generalizes robustly to out-of-distribution environments, achieving 100% goal prediction accuracy in zero-shot, real-world deployment in Outdoor, Forest and Office settings, and an average of 95% checkpoints crossed, demonstrating consistent path following in unseen environments.

</details>


### [143] [Abstention-Aware Personalized Object Rearrangement via Uncertainty-Guided LLM Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17309)
*Sam Collin,Ali Ayub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了APOLLO，一种混合框架，用于具有弃权意识的个性化物体重新排列。该框架结合轻量级个性化嵌入模型（PEM）与选择性大语言模型（LLM）辅助，在CPU上运行，通过不确定性估计仅在模糊决策时调用LLM，从而平衡效率、隐私和推理能力。同时引入APOR合成数据集，实验证明APOLLO在减少LLM使用的同时优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化物体重新排列方法假设干净观测和完全可操作性，未考虑现实环境中杂乱、部分错误的情况，且缺乏对"何时不应该放置物体"的推理能力。

Method: 提出APOLLO混合框架：1) 轻量级个性化嵌入模型（PEM）针对每个用户-环境对少量演示训练，完全在CPU上运行并输出不确定性估计；2) 仅对模糊决策选择性调用LLM进行推理。

Result: 在PARSEC和APOR数据集上，APOLLO优于基于LLM的基线方法，同时大幅减少LLM使用量。

Conclusion: APOLLO通过混合架构在效率、隐私和推理能力之间取得平衡，证明了在现实杂乱环境中进行个性化物体重新排列的可行性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Robotic assistance in household environments requires not only predicting where objects should be placed, but also reasoning about when objects should not be placed at all. Existing approaches to personalized object rearrangement primarily focus on placement decisions under the assumption of clean observations and complete actionability, limiting their applicability in realistic, cluttered, and partially erroneous settings. In this paper, we introduce APOLLO, a hybrid framework for abstention-aware personalized object rearrangement that combines a lightweight, personalized embedding model (PEM) with selective large language model (LLM) assistance. PEM is trained for each user-environment pair using a small number of demonstrations, operates entirely on CPU, and produces uncertainty estimates, which are used to selectively invoke LLM-based reasoning only for ambiguous decisions, balancing efficiency, privacy, and reasoning capability. To evaluate this formulation beyond existing benchmarks, we introduce APOR, a synthetic, LLM-generated dataset that captures room-level, multi-furniture environments, diverse organizational profiles, explicit abstention behavior, and noisy partial scene context. Extensive experiments on both PARSEC and APOR provide initial evidence that APOLLO improves over prior LLM-based baselines in controlled benchmark settings while substantially reducing LLM usage. Code is available at https://github.com/PaInt-Lab/APOLLO.

</details>


### [144] [Transformer-Based Warm-Starting for Feasible and Optimal Terminal Approach to Tumbling Objects with Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17317)
*Yuji Takubo,Maximilian Adang,Mac Schwager,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于因果Transformer的暖启动方法用于空间机械臂接近翻滚目标的实时轨迹生成，通过序列凸规划（SCP）将问题分解为质心平移规划与姿态-机械臂力矩分配两个阶段，在第二阶段利用学习型暖启动减少SCP迭代次数最高28%、运行时间最高23%，且在非凸可行性投影中运行时间几乎减半，同时避免启发式初始化导致的高成本尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨机器人 servicing 的实时轨迹生成面临航天器总线运动、机械臂动力学、可视锥约束与轨迹级安全约束之间的非线性耦合，导致计算瓶颈严重，需要高效方法来加速优化求解。

Method: 将问题分解为系统质心平移规划阶段与耦合的姿态-机械臂力矩分配阶段；对后者采用因果Transformer进行暖启动；比较线性动作解码器与流匹配动作解码器在不同动作分块和训练数据集大小下的表现；通过成本最优SCP和可行性投影两种方式评估暖启动效果。

Result: 在300个保留测试场景中，学习型暖启动使第二阶段SCP迭代次数减少28%、运行时间减少23%，同时保持最终控制成本分布；用于非凸可行性投影时，运行时间相比成本最优SCP几乎减半，且避免了启发式初始化的高成本尾部风险。

Conclusion: 序列模型暖启动能够提升基于优化的终端制导的计算效率和轨迹鲁棒性，为空间操控任务提供了可行的加速方案。

Abstract: Real-time trajectory generation for on-orbit robotic servicing is challenging due to the nonlinear coupling between spacecraft bus motion, manipulator dynamics, visibility cone, and trajectory-level safety constraints. This paper studies learning-based warm-starting for sequential convex programming (SCP) in the terminal approach of a space manipulator toward a tumbling target. The proposed framework decomposes the problem into a system center-of-mass translational planning stage and a coupled attitude--manipulator torque-allocation stage, and applies a causal transformer warm-start to the latter, which constitutes the dominant computational bottleneck. Linear and flow matching action decoders are compared under different action-chunking and training dataset sizes, and the resulting warm-starts are evaluated under both cost-optimal and feasibility projection using SCP. Across 300 held-out scenarios, the learned warm-start reduces the second-stage SCP iteration count by up to 28% and the runtime by 23% while preserving the final control-cost distribution. When the learned warm-starts are used for nonconvex feasibility projection, they nearly halve the runtime relative to cost-optimal SCP, while avoiding the catastrophic high-cost tail behavior observed when initialized heuristically. These results indicate that sequence-model warm-starts can improve both the computational efficiency and trajectory robustness of optimization-based terminal guidance for space manipulation.

</details>


### [145] [Contactless Respiratory Monitoring on Heterogeneous Mobile Robots: A Multimodal Edge-Computing Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17376)
*Milind Rampure,Shadman Sakib,Haley Patel,Zahid Hasan,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构移动机器人的模态自适应无接触呼吸率监测框架，结合亮度自适应传感器选择、关键点引导的胸部ROI提取和信号质量指数滤波机制，在四足和轮式机器人平台上验证了其在多变光照、姿态和距离条件下的泛化能力，RGB和低光相机可达8米，NIR有效至6米，热成像仅适用于短距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧急响应、灾难恢复和传染病场景中，无接触呼吸率监测对减少接触风险至关重要，但现有方法受光照变化、姿态变化、平台异构性以及危险环境中可穿戴传感器不实用等因素制约，缺乏在移动机器人上泛化部署的解决方案。

Method: 提出模态自适应无接触呼吸率监测框架，包括：(1) 亮度自适应传感器选择（RGB、热成像、近红外、低光相机）；(2) 关键点引导的胸部ROI提取以实现姿态鲁棒监测；(3) 基于信号质量指数（SQI）的滤波机制实现可靠呼吸估计。在三种机器人平台（四足和轮式）及多种边缘计算架构上实现与评估。

Result: 框架无需针对平台重新调参即可泛化部署；RGB覆盖最广达8米，NIR有效至6米，热成像仅短距离可靠，低光相机可在完全黑暗环境下工作至8米；揭示了各模态的运营边界。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在移动机器人上实现多模态无接触呼吸率监测的可行性，可作为危险搜救场景中自主分诊和受害者评估的基础。

Abstract: Respiratory-rate (RR) monitoring is a critical component of remote triage and victim assessment in emergency response, disaster recovery, and infectious-disease scenarios, where minimizing physical contact can reduce responder risk and improve operational safety. However, field deployment of contactless RR monitoring remains challenging due to variable illumination, posture changes, platform heterogeneity, and the impracticality of wearable sensors in hazardous environments. In this paper, we present a modality-adaptive contactless RR monitoring framework for heterogeneous mobile robots with onboard edge computing. The proposed system combines brightness-adaptive sensor selection across RGB, thermal, near-infrared (NIR), and low-light cameras, keypoint-guided chest ROI extraction for posture-robust monitoring, and a signal-quality-index (SQI)-based filtering mechanism for reliable respiratory estimation. We implement and evaluate the framework on three robotic platforms spanning quadruped and wheeled locomotion and multiple edge-computing architectures. Experiments conducted across diverse lighting conditions, subject poses, and robot-to-subject distances demonstrate that the framework generalizes across platforms without per-platform algorithmic retuning, while revealing modality-specific operational boundaries. RGB provides the broadest coverage up to 8m, NIR remains effective up to 6m, thermal is reliable only at short range, and low-light sensing supports monitoring in complete darkness up to 8m. Overall, the results demonstrate the feasibility of multimodal contactless RR monitoring on mobile robots and support its use as a foundation for autonomous triage and victim assessment in hazardous search-and-rescue settings.

</details>


### [146] [EgoInfinity: A Web-Scale 4D Hand-Object Interaction Data Engine for Any-View Robot Retargeting and Video-to-Action Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17385)
*Gaotian Wang,Kejia Ren,Andrew Morgan,Yiting Chen,Howard H. Qian,Podshara Chanrungmaneekul,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoInfinity 是一个模块化的4D手-物交互数据引擎，能够将互联网视频自动转化为适用于多种机器人形态的可执行动作数据，无需人工标注，实现了从视频到机器人行为的可扩展桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网视频蕴含丰富的具身操作知识，但现有数据集规模和多样性有限，且将任意RGB视频转化为机器人训练数据的过程缺乏自动化、可扩展的方案。

Method: EgoInfinity 采用模块化引擎设计，集成感知、分割、重建、交互感知优化和重定向模块；通过跨模块度量标定与交互感知优化提升物理可靠性；提出新型运动重定向器，将3D手部运动编译为不同机器人形态的可执行关节轨迹。

Result: 在感知保真度、运动学可行性、接触一致性、跨形态泛化和真实机器人技能获取（抓取、切割、擦拭、倾倒）等多个维度上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: EgoInfinity 为开放世界机器人学习提供了从互联网视频到可执行机器人行为的可扩展桥梁。

Abstract: Internet videos constitute the largest reservoir of embodied human manipulation knowledge, yet converting arbitrary RGB footage into actionable robot training data remains a major bottleneck. Existing lab- or factory-collected datasets are narrow in scale and diversity, limiting open-world robot learning. Instead of proposing a static dataset, we introduce EgoInfinity, a universal 4D hand-object interaction data engine that enables web-scale data generation for robot retargeting and learning. EgoInfinity is a modular engine integrating perception, segmentation, reconstruction, interaction-aware refinement, and retargeting to automate this traditionally unscalable video-to-action problem without human-in-the-loop annotation. Its modular design lets the engine continuously benefit from advances in any incorporated component. With EgoInfinity, in-the-wild human manipulation videos are lifted into agent-agnostic, metric 4D hand-object representations, including hand trajectories, 6-DoF object poses, and contact-relevant states.
